===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.normalizeBlocks(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 98-118
Documentation:
/**
   * Add blocks to IF, WHILE, DO, etc.
   */
Method code:
98 |   private void normalizeBlocks(Node n) {
99 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n)
100 |         && n.getType() != Token.LABEL
101 |         && n.getType() != Token.SWITCH) {
102 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
103 |         if (NodeUtil.isControlStructureCodeBlock(n,c) &&
104 |             c.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
105 |           Node newBlock = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
106 |           newBlock.copyInformationFrom(n);
107 |           n.replaceChild(c, newBlock);
108 |           if (c.getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
109 |             newBlock.addChildrenToFront(c);
110 |           } else {
111 |             newBlock.setWasEmptyNode(true);
112 |           }
113 |           c = newBlock;
114 |           reportChange();
115 |         }
116 |       }
117 |     }
118 |   }

Method code:
155 |   @Override
156 |   public List<GraphEdge<N, E>> getEdges(N n1, N n2) {
157 |     // Since this is a method from a generic graph, edges from both
158 |     // directions must be added to the returning list.
159 |     List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> forwardEdges = getDirectedGraphEdges(n1, n2);
160 |     List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> backwardEdges = getDirectedGraphEdges(n2, n1);
161 |     int totalSize = forwardEdges.size() + backwardEdges.size();
162 |     List<GraphEdge<N, E>> edges = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(totalSize);
163 |     edges.addAll(forwardEdges);
164 |     edges.addAll(backwardEdges);
165 |     return edges;
166 |   }

===== M0009 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 441-461
Documentation:

Method code:
441 |     @Override
442 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
443 |       if (lineBreak) {
444 |         if (sawFunction) {
445 |           startNewLine();
446 |           sawFunction = false;
447 |         }
448 |       }
449 | 
450 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
451 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
452 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
453 |       int len = code.length();
454 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
455 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
456 |         if (ch == ';') {
457 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
458 |         }
459 |       }
460 |       maybeCutLine();
461 |     }

===== M0010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlStructureCheck.check(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlStructureCheck.java
Original range: 52-74
Documentation:
/**
   * Reports errors for any invalid use of control structures.
   *
   * @param node Current node to check.
   */
Method code:
52 |   private void check(Node node) {
53 |     switch (node.getType()) {
54 |       case Token.WITH:
55 |         JSDocInfo info = node.getJSDocInfo();
56 |         boolean allowWith =
57 |             info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("with");
58 |         if (!allowWith) {
59 |           report(node, USE_OF_WITH);
60 |         }
61 |         break;
62 | 
63 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
64 |         // Remember the source file name in case we need to report an error.
65 |         sourceName = (String) node.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
66 |         break;
67 |     }
68 | 
69 |     for (Node bChild = node.getFirstChild(); bChild != null;) {
70 |       Node next = bChild.getNext();
71 |       check(bChild);
72 |       bChild = next;
73 |     }
74 |   }

795 |     void addRef(Ref ref) {
796 |       switch (ref.type) {
797 |         case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
798 |           if (declaration == null) {
799 |             declaration = ref;
800 |             docInfo = getDocInfoForDeclaration(ref);

805 |           break;

809 |           break;

811 |         case DIRECT_GET:
812 |           addRefInternal(ref);
813 |           totalGets++;
814 |           break;

817 |           aliasingGets++;

819 |           break;

825 |         default:

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 311-318
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
311 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
312 |     if (c <= 127) {
313 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
314 |     } else {
315 |       return c == 0xA0
316 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
317 |     }
318 |   }

===== M0019 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1543-1577
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1543 |     public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1544 |         if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1545 |             try {
1546 |                 int[] indices1 = (int[])getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1547 |                 int[] indices2 = (int[])node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1548 |                 if (indices1 == null) {
1549 |                     if (indices2 != null)
1550 |                         return false;
1551 |                 } else if (indices2 == null) {
1552 |                     return false;
1553 |                 } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1554 |                     return false;
1555 |                 } else {
1556 |                     for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1557 |                         if (indices1[i] != indices2[i])
1558 |                             return false;
1559 |                     }
1560 |                 }
1561 |             } catch (Exception e) {
1562 |                 return false;
1563 |             }
1564 |         } else if (type == Token.INC ||
1565 |                    type == Token.DEC) {
1566 |             int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1567 |             int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1568 |             if (post1 != post2)
1569 |                 return false;
1570 |         } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1571 |             int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1572 |             int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1573 |             if (quoted1 != quoted2)
1574 |                 return false;
1575 |         }
1576 |         return true;
1577 |     }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.Graph.connectIfNotFound(N,E,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/Graph.java
Original range: 121-138
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects two nodes in the graph with an edge if such edge does not already
   * exists between the nodes.
   *
   * @param n1 First node.
   * @param edge The edge.
   * @param n2 Second node.
   */
Method code:
121 |   public final void connectIfNotFound(N n1, E edge, N n2) {
122 |     GraphNode<N, E> node1 = getNode(n1);
123 |     GraphNode<N, E> node2 = getNode(n2);
124 |     if (node1 == null) {
125 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(n1 + " does not exist in graph");
126 |     }
127 |     if (node2 == null) {
128 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(n2 + " does not exist in graph");
129 |     }
130 |     List<GraphEdge<N, E>> edgeList = getEdges(n1, n2);
131 |     for (GraphEdge<N, E> e : edgeList) {
132 |       // Check == first to short circuit nulls.
133 |       if (e.getValue() == edge || e.getValue().equals(edge)) {
134 |         return;
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 |     connect(n1, edge, n2);
138 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.LoopInternal.optimizePasses()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 361-380
Documentation:
/** Re-arrange the passes in an optimal order. */
Method code:
361 |     private void optimizePasses() {
362 |       // It's important that this ordering is deterministic, so that
363 |       // multiple compiles with the same input produce exactly the same
364 |       // results.
365 |       //
366 |       // To do this, grab any passes we recognize, and move them to the end
367 |       // in an "optimal" order.
368 |       List<NamedPass> optimalPasses = Lists.newArrayList();
369 |       for (String passName : OPTIMAL_ORDER) {
370 |         for (NamedPass pass : myPasses) {
371 |           if (pass.name.equals(passName)) {
372 |             optimalPasses.add(pass);
373 |             break;
374 |           }
375 |         }
376 |       }
377 | 
378 |       myPasses.removeAll(optimalPasses);
379 |       myPasses.addAll(optimalPasses);
380 |     }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AbstractCommandLineRunner.createDefineReplacements(List<String>,CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AbstractCommandLineRunner.java
Original range: 850-897
Documentation:
/**
   * Create a map of constant names to constant values from a textual
   * description of the map.
   *
   * @param definitions A list of overriding definitions for defines in
   *     the form <name>[=<val>], where <val> is a number, boolean, or
   *     single-quoted string without single quotes.
   */
Method code:
850 |   @VisibleForTesting
851 |   static void createDefineReplacements(List<String> definitions,
852 |       CompilerOptions options) {
853 |     // Parse the definitions
854 |     for (String override : definitions) {
855 |       String[] assignment = override.split("=", 2);
856 |       String defName = assignment[0];
857 | 
858 |       if (defName.length() > 0) {
859 |         if (assignment.length == 1) {
860 |           options.setDefineToBooleanLiteral(defName, true);
861 |           continue;
862 |         } else {
863 |           String defValue = assignment[1];
864 | 
865 |           if (defValue.equals("true")) {
866 |             options.setDefineToBooleanLiteral(defName, true);
867 |             continue;
868 |           } else if (defValue.equals("false")) {
869 |             options.setDefineToBooleanLiteral(defName, false);
870 |             continue;
871 |           } else if (defValue.length() > 1 &&
872 |               defValue.charAt(0) == '\'' &&
873 |               defValue.charAt(defValue.length() - 1) == '\'') {
874 |             // If the value starts and ends with a single quote,
875 |             // we assume that it's a string.
876 |             String maybeStringVal =
877 |                 defValue.substring(1, defValue.length() - 1);
878 |             if (maybeStringVal.indexOf('\'') == -1) {
879 |               options.setDefineToStringLiteral(defName, maybeStringVal);
880 |               continue;
881 |             }
882 |           } else {
883 |             try {
884 |               options.setDefineToDoubleLiteral(defName,
885 |                   Double.parseDouble(defValue));
886 |               continue;
887 |             } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
888 |               // do nothing, it will be caught at the end
889 |             }
890 |           }
891 |         }
892 |       }
893 | 
894 |       throw new RuntimeException(
895 |           "--define flag syntax invalid: " + override);
896 |     }
897 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 510-562
Documentation:

Method code:
510 |    @Override
511 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
512 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
513 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
514 |       if (name == null) {
515 |         name = new Name();
516 |         name.setIdentifier("");
517 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
518 |       }
519 |       Node node = new com.google.javascript.rhino.FunctionNode(
520 |           name.getIdentifier());
521 |       node.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, functionNode.getSourceName());
522 |       Node newName = transform(name);
523 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
524 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
525 |         // declaration.
526 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
527 |         // TODO(user) Mark line number of paren correctly.
528 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
529 |         // same line as the function keyword.
530 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
531 |             functionNode.getLp();
532 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
536 |       Node lp = new Node(Token.LP);
537 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
538 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
539 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
540 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
541 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
542 |       // TODO(user): Mark line number of paren correctly.
543 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
544 |       if (fnName != null) {
545 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
546 |       } else {
547 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
548 |       }
549 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
550 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
551 | 
552 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
553 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
554 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
555 |       }
556 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
557 | 
558 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
559 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
560 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
561 |      return node;
562 |     }

===== M0075 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckUnreachableCode.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckUnreachableCode.java
Original range: 53-76
Documentation:

Method code:
53 |   @Override
54 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
55 |     GraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = t.getControlFlowGraph().getNode(n);
56 |     if (gNode != null && gNode.getAnnotation() != GraphReachability.REACHABLE) {
57 | 
58 |       // Only report error when there are some line number informations.
59 |       // There are synthetic nodes with no line number informations, nodes
60 |       // introduce by other passes (although not likely since this pass should
61 |       // be executed early) or some rhino bug.
62 |       if (n.getLineno() != -1 &&
63 |           // Allow spurious semi-colons and spurious breaks.
64 |           n.getType() != Token.EMPTY && n.getType() != Token.BREAK) {
65 |         compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, level, UNREACHABLE_CODE));
66 |         // From now on, we are going to assume the user fixed the error and not
67 |         // give more warning related to code section reachable from this node.
68 |         new GraphReachability<Node, ControlFlowGraph.Branch>(
69 |             t.getControlFlowGraph()).recompute(n);
70 | 
71 |         // Saves time by not traversing children.
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 |     return true;
76 |   }

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractBlockComment(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1215-1260
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1215 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1216 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1217 | 
1218 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1219 | 
1220 |     do {
1221 |       switch (token) {
1222 |         case ANNOTATION:
1223 |         case EOC:
1224 |         case EOF:
1225 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1226 | 
1227 |         case STAR:
1228 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1229 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1230 |               builder.append(' ');
1231 |             }
1232 | 
1233 |             builder.append('*');
1234 |           }
1235 | 
1236 |           token = next();
1237 |           continue;
1238 | 
1239 |         case EOL:
1240 |           ignoreStar = true;
1241 |           builder.append('\n');
1242 |           token = next();
1243 |           continue;
1244 | 
1245 |         default:
1246 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1247 |             builder.append(' ');
1248 |           }
1249 | 
1250 |           ignoreStar = false;
1251 | 
1252 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1253 | 
1254 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1255 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1256 |           builder.append(line);
1257 |           token = next();
1258 |       }
1259 |     } while (true);
1260 |   }

===== M0084 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1635-1661
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold array-length. e.g [1, 2, 3].length ==> 3, [x, y].length ==> 2
   */
Method code:
1635 |   void tryFoldGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right,
1636 |                       Node parent) {
1637 |     if (right.getType() == Token.STRING &&
1638 |         right.getString().equals("length")) {
1639 |       int knownLength = -1;
1640 |       switch (left.getType()) {
1641 |         case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1642 |           if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
1643 |             // Nope, can't fold this, without handling the side-effects.
1644 |             return;
1645 |           }
1646 |           knownLength = left.getChildCount();
1647 |           break;
1648 |         case Token.STRING:
1649 |           knownLength = left.getString().length();
1650 |           break;
1651 |         default:
1652 |           // Not a foldable case, forget it.
1653 |           return;
1654 |       }
1655 | 
1656 |       Preconditions.checkState(knownLength != -1);
1657 |       Node lengthNode = Node.newNumber(knownLength);
1658 |       parent.replaceChild(n, lengthNode);
1659 |       t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1660 |     }
1661 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 138-791
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
138 |   boolean parse() {
139 |     int lineno;
140 |     int charno;
141 | 
142 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
143 |     JSTypeExpression type;
144 | 
145 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
146 |     JsDocToken token = next();
147 | 
148 |     ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
149 |     token = blockInfo.token;
150 | 
151 |     // If we have a block level comment, record it.
152 |     if (blockInfo.string.length() > 0) {
153 |       jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
157 |     retry: for (;;) {
158 |       switch (token) {
159 |         case ANNOTATION:
160 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
161 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
162 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
163 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
164 | 
165 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
166 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
167 |             if (annotation == null) {
168 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
169 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
170 |             } else {
171 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
172 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
173 | 
174 |               switch (annotation) {
175 |                 case AUTHOR:
176 |                   ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
177 |                   String author = authorInfo.string;
178 | 
179 |                   if (author.length() == 0) {
180 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
181 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
182 |                   } else {
183 |                     jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
184 |                   }
185 | 
186 |                   token = authorInfo.token;
187 |                   continue retry;
188 | 
189 |                 case CONSTANT:
190 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
191 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
192 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
193 |                   }
194 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
195 |                   continue retry;
196 | 
197 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
198 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
199 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
200 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
201 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
202 |                     } else {
203 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
204 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
205 |                     }
206 |                   }
207 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
208 |                   continue retry;
209 | 
210 |                 case DEPRECATED:
211 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
212 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
213 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
214 |                   }
215 | 
216 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
217 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
218 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
219 | 
220 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
221 | 
222 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
223 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
224 |                   }
225 | 
226 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
227 |                   continue retry;
228 | 
229 |                 case INTERFACE:
230 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
231 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
232 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
233 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
234 |                     } else {
235 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
236 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
237 |                     }
238 |                   }
239 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
240 |                   continue retry;
241 | 
242 |                 case DESC:
243 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
244 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
245 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
246 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
247 |                     continue retry;
248 |                   } else {
249 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
250 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
251 | 
252 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
253 | 
254 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
255 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
256 |                     continue retry;
257 |                   }
258 | 
259 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
260 |                   ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
261 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
262 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
263 | 
264 |                   String fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
265 | 
266 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
267 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
268 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
269 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
270 |                   }
271 |                   token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
272 |                   continue retry;
273 | 
274 |                 case LICENSE:
275 |                 case PRESERVE:
276 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
277 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
278 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
279 | 
280 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
281 | 
282 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
283 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
284 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
285 |                     }
286 |                   }
287 | 
288 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
289 |                   continue retry;
290 | 
291 |                 case ENUM:
292 |                   token = next();
293 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
294 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
295 | 
296 |                   type = null;
297 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
298 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
299 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
300 |                   }
301 | 
302 |                   if (type == null) {
303 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
304 |                   }
305 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
306 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
307 |                   }
308 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
309 |                   continue retry;
310 | 
311 |                 case EXPORT:
312 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
313 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
314 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
315 |                   }
316 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
317 |                   continue retry;
318 | 
319 |                 case EXTERNS:
320 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
321 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
322 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
323 |                   }
324 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
325 |                   continue retry;
326 | 
327 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
328 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
329 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
330 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
331 |                   }
332 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
333 |                   continue retry;
334 | 
335 |                 case EXTENDS:
336 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
337 |                   skipEOLs();
338 |                   token = next();
339 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
340 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
341 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
342 | 
343 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
344 |                     token = next();
345 |                     matchingRc = true;
346 |                   }
347 | 
348 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
349 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
350 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
351 | 
352 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
353 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
354 | 
355 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
356 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
357 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
358 |                     }
359 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
360 | 
361 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
362 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {
363 |                         parser.addWarning(
364 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
365 |                       }
366 |                     } else {
367 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
368 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
369 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
370 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
371 |                             lineno, charno);
372 |                       }
373 |                     }
374 |                     token = next();
375 |                     if (matchingRc) {
376 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
377 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
378 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
379 |                       }
380 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
381 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
382 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
383 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
384 |                     }
385 |                   } else {
386 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
387 |                   }
388 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
389 |                   continue retry;
390 | 
391 |                 case HIDDEN:
392 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
393 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
394 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
395 |                   }
396 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
397 |                   continue retry;
398 | 
399 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
400 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
401 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
402 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
403 |                   }
404 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
405 |                   continue retry;
406 | 
407 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
408 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
409 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
410 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
411 |                   }
412 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
413 |                   continue retry;
414 | 
415 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
416 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
417 |                   continue retry;
418 | 
419 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
420 |                 case OVERRIDE:
421 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
422 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
423 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
424 |                   }
425 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
426 |                   continue retry;
427 | 
428 |                 case THROWS:
429 |                   skipEOLs();
430 |                   token = next();
431 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
432 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
433 |                   type = null;
434 | 
435 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
436 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
437 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
438 | 
439 |                     if (type == null) {
440 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
441 |                       // recovering parsing
442 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
443 |                       continue retry;
444 |                     }
445 |                   }
446 | 
447 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
448 |                   token = current();
449 | 
450 |                   // Save the throw type.
451 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
452 | 
453 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
454 |                   ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
455 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
456 | 
457 |                   String description = descriptionInfo.string;
458 | 
459 |                   if (description.length() > 0) {
460 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
461 |                   }
462 | 
463 |                   token = descriptionInfo.token;
464 |                   continue retry;
465 | 
466 |                 case PARAM:
467 |                   skipEOLs();
468 |                   token = next();
469 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
470 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
471 |                   type = null;
472 | 
473 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
474 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
475 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
476 | 
477 |                     if (type == null) {
478 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
479 |                       // recovering parsing
480 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
481 |                       continue retry;
482 |                     }
483 |                     skipEOLs();
484 |                     token = next();
485 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
486 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
487 |                   }
488 | 
489 |                   String name = null;
490 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
491 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
492 |                     token = next();
493 |                   }
494 | 
495 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
496 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
497 |                         lineno, charno);
498 |                   } else {
499 |                     name = stream.getString();
500 | 
501 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
502 |                       token = next();
503 | 
504 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
505 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
506 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
507 |                         token = next();
508 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
509 |                           token = next();
510 |                         }
511 |                       }
512 | 
513 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
514 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
515 |                       } else if (type != null) {
516 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
517 |                         // already.
518 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
519 |                       }
520 |                     }
521 | 
522 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
523 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
524 |                     // for handling properties of params.
525 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
526 |                       name = null;
527 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
528 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
529 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
530 |                             lineno, charno);
531 |                       } else {
532 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
533 |                             lineno, charno);
534 |                       }
535 |                     }
536 |                   }
537 | 
538 |                   if (name == null) {
539 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
540 |                     continue retry;
541 |                   }
542 | 
543 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
544 | 
545 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
546 |                   ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
547 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
548 | 
549 |                   String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
550 | 
551 |                   if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
552 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
553 |                                                             paramDescription);
554 |                   }
555 | 
556 |                   token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
557 |                   continue retry;
558 | 
559 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
560 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
561 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
562 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
563 |                   }
564 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
565 |                   continue retry;
566 | 
567 |                 case PRIVATE:
568 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
569 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
570 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
571 |                   }
572 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
573 |                   continue retry;
574 | 
575 |                 case PROTECTED:
576 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
577 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
578 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
579 |                   }
580 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
581 |                   continue retry;
582 | 
583 |                 case PUBLIC:
584 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
585 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
586 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
587 |                   }
588 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
589 |                   continue retry;
590 | 
591 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
592 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
593 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
594 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
595 |                   }
596 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
597 |                   continue retry;
598 | 
599 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
600 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
601 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
602 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
603 |                   }
604 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
605 |                   continue retry;
606 | 
607 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
608 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
609 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
610 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
611 |                   }
612 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
613 |                   continue retry;
614 | 
615 |                 case SEE:
616 |                   ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
617 |                   String reference = referenceInfo.string;
618 | 
619 |                   if (reference.length() == 0) {
620 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
621 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
622 |                   } else {
623 |                     jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
624 |                   }
625 | 
626 |                   token = referenceInfo.token;
627 |                   continue retry;
628 | 
629 |                 case SUPPRESS:
630 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
631 |                   continue retry;
632 | 
633 |                 case TEMPLATE:
634 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
635 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
636 | 
637 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
638 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
639 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
640 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
641 |                       templateTypeName)) {
642 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
643 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
644 |                   }
645 | 
646 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
647 |                   continue retry;
648 | 
649 |                 case VERSION:
650 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
651 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
652 | 
653 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
654 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
655 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
656 |                   } else {
657 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
658 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
659 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
660 |                     }
661 |                   }
662 | 
663 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
664 |                   continue retry;
665 | 
666 |                 case DEFINE:
667 |                 case RETURN:
668 |                 case THIS:
669 |                 case TYPE:
670 |                 case TYPEDEF:
671 |                   skipEOLs();
672 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
673 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
674 | 
675 |                   token = next();
676 | 
677 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
678 | 
679 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
680 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
681 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
682 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
683 |                     }
684 |                   }
685 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
686 | 
687 |                   if (type == null) {
688 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
689 |                     // recovering parsing
690 |                   } else {
691 |                     switch (annotation) {
692 |                       case DEFINE:
693 |                         if (!isValidDefineType(typeNode)) {
694 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define.badtype",
695 |                               lineno, charno);
696 |                         } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
697 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
698 |                               lineno, charno);
699 |                         }
700 |                         break;
701 | 
702 |                       case RETURN:
703 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
704 |                           parser.addWarning(
705 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
706 |                           break;
707 |                         }
708 | 
709 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
710 |                         token = current();
711 | 
712 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
713 |                         ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
714 |                             extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
715 | 
716 |                         String returnDescription = returnDescriptionInfo.string;
717 | 
718 |                         if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
719 |                           jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
720 |                               returnDescription);
721 |                         }
722 | 
723 |                         token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
724 |                         continue retry;
725 | 
726 |                       case THIS:
727 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
728 |                           parser.addWarning(
729 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
730 |                         }
731 |                         break;
732 | 
733 |                       case TYPE:
734 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
735 |                           parser.addWarning(
736 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
737 |                         }
738 |                         break;
739 | 
740 |                       case TYPEDEF:
741 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
742 |                           parser.addWarning(
743 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
744 |                         }
745 |                         break;
746 |                     }
747 | 
748 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
749 |                   }
750 |                   continue retry;
751 |               }
752 |             }
753 |           }
754 |           break;
755 | 
756 |         case EOC:
757 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
758 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
759 |           }
760 | 
761 |           return true;
762 | 
763 |         case EOF:
764 |           // discard any accumulated information
765 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
766 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
767 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
768 |           return false;
769 | 
770 |         case EOL:
771 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
772 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
773 |           }
774 |           token = next();
775 |           continue retry;
776 | 
777 |         default:
778 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
779 |             token = next();
780 |             continue retry;
781 |           } else {
782 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
783 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
784 |             continue retry;
785 |           }
786 |       }
787 | 
788 |       // next token
789 |       token = next();
790 |     }
791 |   }

===== M0100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 421-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
421 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
422 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
423 | 
424 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
425 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
426 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
427 |       return 0;
428 |     }
429 | 
430 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
431 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
432 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
433 |       // a new statistic may have been added
434 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
435 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
436 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
441 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
442 |       return 0;
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
446 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
447 |   }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 354-480
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
354 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
355 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects) {
356 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
357 |     // that we know to be safe
358 |     switch (n.getType()) {
359 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
360 |       case Token.AND:
361 |       case Token.BLOCK:
362 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
363 |       case Token.HOOK:
364 |       case Token.IF:
365 |       case Token.IN:
366 |       case Token.LP:
367 |       case Token.NUMBER:
368 |       case Token.OR:
369 |       case Token.THIS:
370 |       case Token.TRUE:
371 |       case Token.FALSE:
372 |       case Token.NULL:
373 |       case Token.STRING:
374 |       case Token.SWITCH:
375 |       case Token.TRY:
376 |       case Token.EMPTY:
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
380 |       case Token.THROW:
381 |         return true;
382 | 
383 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
384 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
385 |       case Token.REGEXP:
386 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
387 |           return true;
388 |         }
389 |         break;
390 | 
391 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
392 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
393 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null)
394 |           return true;
395 | 
396 |         break;
397 | 
398 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
399 |         // Anonymous functions don't have side-effects, but named ones
400 |         // change the namespace. Therefore, we check if the function has
401 |         // a name. Either way, we don't need to check the children, since
402 |         // they aren't executed at declaration time.
403 |         //
404 |         return !isFunctionAnonymous(n);
405 | 
406 |       case Token.NEW:
407 |         {
408 |           if (checkForNewObjects) {
409 |             return true;
410 |           }
411 | 
412 |           // calls to constructors that have no side effects have the
413 |           // no side effect property set.
414 |           if (n.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
415 |             break;
416 |           }
417 | 
418 |           // certain constructors are certified side effect free
419 |           Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
420 |           if (Token.NAME == constructor.getType()) {
421 |             String className = constructor.getString();
422 |             if (CONSTRUCTORS_WITHOUT_SIDE_EFFECTS.contains(className)) {
423 |               // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
424 |               // side-effects
425 |               break;
426 |             }
427 |           } else {
428 |             // the constructor could also be an expression like
429 |             // new (useArray ? Object : Array)();
430 |           }
431 |         }
432 |         return true;
433 | 
434 |       case Token.CALL:
435 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
436 |         // side effect property set.
437 |         if (n.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
438 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
439 |           // side-effects
440 |           break;
441 |         }
442 |         return true;
443 | 
444 |       default:
445 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType()))
446 |           break;
447 | 
448 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
449 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
450 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
451 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
452 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
453 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
454 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects) ||
455 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
456 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects)) {
457 |             return true;
458 |           }
459 | 
460 |           Node current = n.getFirstChild();
461 |           for (;
462 |                current.getType() == Token.GETPROP ||
463 |                current.getType() == Token.GETELEM;
464 |                current = current.getFirstChild()) { }
465 | 
466 |           return !(isLiteralValue(current) ||
467 |               current.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
468 |         }
469 | 
470 |         return true;
471 |     }
472 | 
473 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
474 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects)) {
475 |         return true;
476 |       }
477 |     }
478 | 
479 |     return false;
480 |   }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 192-211
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
192 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
193 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
194 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
195 |     worklist.add(entry);
196 | 
197 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
198 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
199 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
200 |         continue;
201 |       }
202 | 
203 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
204 | 
205 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
206 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
207 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
208 |         worklist.add(candidate);
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 |   }

45 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
46 |     switch (n.getType()) {
47 |       case Token.GETELEM:
48 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
49 |         Node right = left.getNext();

51 |             NodeUtil.isValidPropertyName(right.getString())) {

===== M0110 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getChildBefore(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 538-548
Documentation:

Method code:
538 |     public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
539 |         if (child == first)
540 |             return null;
541 |         Node n = first;
542 |         while (n.next != child) {
543 |             n = n.next;

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 60-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
60 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
61 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
62 |     int c;
63 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
64 |     for (;;) {
65 |       // eat white spaces
66 |       for (;;) {
67 |         charno = -1;
68 |         c = getChar();
69 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
71 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
72 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
73 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
74 |           break;
75 |         }
76 |       }
77 | 
78 |       switch (c) {
79 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
80 |         case '@':
81 |           do {
82 |             c = getChar();
83 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
84 |               addToString(c);
85 |             } else {
86 |               ungetChar(c);
87 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
88 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
89 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
90 |             }
91 |           } while (true);
92 | 
93 |         case '*':
94 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
95 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
96 |           } else {
97 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
98 |           }
99 | 
100 |         case ',':
101 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
102 | 
103 |         case '>':
104 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
105 | 
106 |         case '(':
107 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
108 | 
109 |         case ')':
110 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
111 | 
112 |         case '{':
113 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
114 | 
115 |         case '}':
116 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
117 | 
118 |         case '[':
119 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
120 | 
121 |         case ']':
122 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
123 | 
124 |         case '?':
125 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
126 | 
127 |         case '!':
128 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
129 | 
130 |         case ':':
131 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
132 | 
133 |         case '=':
134 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
135 | 
136 |         case '|':
137 |           matchChar('|');
138 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
139 | 
140 |         case '.':
141 |           c = getChar();
142 |           if (c == '<') {
143 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
144 |           } else {
145 |             if (c == '.') {
146 |               c = getChar();
147 |               if (c == '.') {
148 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
149 |               } else {
150 |                 addToString('.');
151 |               }
152 |             }
153 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
154 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
155 |             c = '.';
156 |           }
157 |           // fall through
158 | 
159 |         default: {
160 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
161 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
162 |           int c1 = c;
163 |           addToString(c);
164 |           int c2 = getChar();
165 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
166 |             ungetChar(c2);
167 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
168 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
169 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
170 |           } else {
171 |             do {
172 |               c1 = c2;
173 |               c2 = getChar();
174 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
175 |                 ungetChar(c2);
176 |                 ungetChar(c1);
177 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
178 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
179 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
180 |               } else {
181 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                 } else {
184 |                   ungetChar(c2);
185 |                   addToString(c1);
186 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
187 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
188 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
189 |                 }
190 |               }
191 |             } while (true);
192 |           }
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0113 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1614-1624
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1614 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1615 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1616 |     int len = s.length();
1617 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1618 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1619 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1620 |         return false;
1621 |       }
1622 |     }
1623 |     return true;
1624 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldAssign(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 440-501
Documentation:

Method code:
440 |   private void tryFoldAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right) {
441 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN);
442 | 
443 |     // Tries to convert x = x + y -> x += y;
444 |     if (!right.hasChildren() ||
445 |         right.getFirstChild().getNext() != right.getLastChild()) {
446 |       // RHS must have two children.
447 |       return;
448 |     }
449 | 
450 |     if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
451 |       return;
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     Node leftChild = right.getFirstChild();
455 |     if (!compiler.areNodesEqualForInlining(left, leftChild)) {
456 |       return;
457 |     }
458 | 
459 |     int newType = -1;
460 |     switch (right.getType()) {
461 |       case Token.ADD:
462 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_ADD;
463 |         break;
464 |       case Token.BITAND:
465 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITAND;
466 |         break;
467 |       case Token.BITOR:
468 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITOR;
469 |         break;
470 |       case Token.BITXOR:
471 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR;
472 |         break;
473 |       case Token.DIV:
474 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_DIV;
475 |         break;
476 |       case Token.LSH:
477 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_LSH;
478 |         break;
479 |       case Token.MOD:
480 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MOD;
481 |         break;
482 |       case Token.MUL:
483 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MUL;
484 |         break;
485 |       case Token.RSH:
486 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_RSH;
487 |         break;
488 |       case Token.SUB:
489 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_SUB;
490 |         break;
491 |       case Token.URSH:
492 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_URSH;
493 |         break;
494 |       default:
495 |         return;
496 |     }
497 | 
498 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, new Node(newType,
499 |         left.detachFromParent(), right.getLastChild().detachFromParent()));
500 |     t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
501 |   }

===== M0120 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runInCompilerThread(Callable<T>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 489-539
Documentation:

Method code:
489 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
490 |   private <T> T runInCompilerThread(final Callable<T> callable) {
491 | 
492 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
493 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
494 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
495 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
496 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
497 | 
498 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = options.tracer.isOn();
499 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
500 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
501 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
502 |       public void run() {
503 |         try {
504 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
505 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
506 |           }
507 |           result[0] = callable.call();
508 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
509 |           exception[0] = e;
510 |         } finally {
511 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
512 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
513 |           }
514 |         }
515 |       }
516 |     };
517 | 
518 |     if (useThreads) {
519 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
520 |       th.start();
521 |       while (true) {
522 |         try {
523 |           th.join();
524 |           break;
525 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
526 |           // ignore
527 |         }
528 |       }
529 |     } else {
530 |       runnable.run();
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
534 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
535 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
536 |     }
537 | 
538 |     return (T) result[0];
539 |   }

/**
   * Registers ProvidedNames for prefix namespaces if they haven't
   * already been defined. The prefix namespaces must be registered in
   * order from shortest to longest.
   *
   * @param ns The namespace whose prefixes may need to be provided.
   * @param node The EXPR of the provide call.
   * @param module The current module.
   */
Method code:
638 |   private void registerAnyProvidedPrefixes(
639 |       String ns, Node node, JSModule module) {
640 |     int pos = ns.indexOf('.');
641 |     while (pos != -1) {
642 |       String prefixNs = ns.substring(0, pos);
643 |       pos = ns.indexOf('.', pos + 1);
644 |       if (providedNames.containsKey(prefixNs)) {
645 |         providedNames.get(prefixNs).addProvide(
646 |             node, module, false /* implicit */);
647 |       } else {
648 |         providedNames.put(
649 |             prefixNs,
650 |             new ProvidedName(prefixNs, node, module, false /* implicit */));
651 |       }
652 |     }
653 |   }

===== M0135 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 410-432
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
410 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
411 |       switch (n.getType()) {
412 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
413 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
414 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
415 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
416 |         case Token.OR:
417 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
418 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
419 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
420 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
421 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
422 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
423 |         case Token.HOOK:
424 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
425 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
426 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
427 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
428 |           Node third = second.getNext();
429 |           return getValueType(third);
430 |       }
431 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
432 |     }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 177-207
Documentation:

Method code:
177 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
178 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
179 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;
180 |     if (jsDoc != null) {
181 |       nodeWithJsDoc = new NodeWithJsDoc();
182 |       nodesWithJsDoc.put(jsDoc, nodeWithJsDoc);
183 |     }
184 | 
185 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
186 |     if (nodeWithJsDoc != null) {
187 |       nodeWithJsDoc.node = irNode;
188 |     }
189 | 
190 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
191 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
192 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
193 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
194 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
195 |     } else {
196 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
197 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
198 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
199 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
200 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
201 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
202 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
203 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
204 |       }
205 |     }
206 |     return irNode;
207 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 79-109
Documentation:

Method code:
79 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
80 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
82 | 
83 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
84 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
85 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
86 | 
87 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
88 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
89 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
90 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n)) {
91 |         declareVar(fnName, fnNameNode, n, null, null, n);
92 |       }
93 | 
94 |       // Args: Declare function variables
95 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
96 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
97 |            a = a.getNext()) {
98 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
99 |         declareVar(a.getString(), a, args, n, null, n);
100 |       }
101 | 
102 |       // Body
103 |       scanVars(body, n);
104 |     } else {
105 |       // It's the global block
106 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
107 |       scanVars(n, null);
108 |     }
109 |   }

===== M0167 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.ProvidedName.replace()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 744-806
Documentation:
/**
     * Replace the provide statement.
     *
     * If we're providing a name with no definition, then create one.
     * If we're providing a name with a duplicate definition, then make sure
     * that definition becomes a declaration.
     */
Method code:
744 |     void replace() {
745 |       if (firstNode == null) {
746 |         // Don't touch the base case ('goog').
747 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
748 |         return;
749 |       }
750 | 
751 |       // Handle the case where there is a duplicate definition for an explicitly
752 |       // provided symbol.
753 |       if (candidateDefinition != null && explicitNode != null) {
754 |         explicitNode.detachFromParent();
755 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
756 | 
757 |         // Does this need a VAR keyword?
758 |         replacementNode = candidateDefinition;
759 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(candidateDefinition)) {
760 |           candidateDefinition.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE, true);
761 |           Node assignNode = candidateDefinition.getFirstChild();
762 |           Node nameNode = assignNode.getFirstChild();
763 |           if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
764 |             // Need to convert this assign to a var declaration.
765 |             Node valueNode = nameNode.getNext();
766 |             assignNode.removeChild(nameNode);
767 |             assignNode.removeChild(valueNode);
768 |             nameNode.addChildToFront(valueNode);
769 |             Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
770 |             varNode.copyInformationFrom(candidateDefinition);
771 |             candidateDefinition.getParent().replaceChild(
772 |                 candidateDefinition, varNode);
773 |             nameNode.setJSDocInfo(assignNode.getJSDocInfo());
774 |             compiler.reportCodeChange();
775 |             replacementNode = varNode;
776 |           }
777 |         }
778 |       } else {
779 |         // Handle the case where there's not a duplicate definition.
780 |         replacementNode = createDeclarationNode();
781 |         if (firstModule == minimumModule) {
782 |           firstNode.getParent().addChildBefore(replacementNode, firstNode);
783 |         } else {
784 |           // In this case, the name was implicitly provided by two independent
785 |           // modules. We need to move this code up to a common module.
786 |           int indexOfDot = namespace.indexOf('.');
787 |           if (indexOfDot == -1) {
788 |             // Any old place is fine.
789 |             compiler.getNodeForCodeInsertion(minimumModule)
790 |                 .addChildToBack(replacementNode);
791 |           } else {
792 |             // Add it after the parent namespace.
793 |             ProvidedName parentName =
794 |                 providedNames.get(namespace.substring(0, indexOfDot));
795 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName);
796 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(parentName.replacementNode);
797 |             parentName.replacementNode.getParent().addChildAfter(
798 |                 replacementNode, parentName.replacementNode);
799 |           }
800 |         }
801 |         if (explicitNode != null) {
802 |           explicitNode.detachFromParent();
803 |         }
804 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
805 |       }
806 |     }

===== M0179 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 483-516
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
483 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
484 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
485 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
486 | 
487 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
488 | 
489 |     boolean anonymous = parent != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n);
490 | 
491 |     if (!anonymous) {
492 |       // Named functions are parent of the containing scope.
493 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     curNode = n;
497 |     pushScope(n);
498 | 
499 |     if (anonymous) {
500 |       // Anonymous function names are parent of the contained scope.
501 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
505 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
506 | 
507 |     // Args
508 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
509 | 
510 |     // Body
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
512 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
513 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
514 | 
515 |     popScope();
516 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.addChildBefore(Node,Node)

Original range: 622-637
Documentation:
/**
     * Add 'child' before 'node'.
     */
Method code:
622 |     public void addChildBefore(Node newChild, Node node) {
623 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(node != null,
624 |             "The existing child node of the parent should not be null.");
625 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
626 |             "The new child node has siblings.");
627 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
628 |             "The new child node already has a parent.");
629 |         if (first == node) {
630 |             newChild.parent = this;
631 |             newChild.next = first;
632 |             first = newChild;
633 |             return;
634 |         }
635 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(node);
636 |         addChildAfter(newChild, prev);
637 |     }
