===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 80-106
Documentation:

Method code:
80 |   @Override
81 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
82 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
83 |         new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange));
84 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
85 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
86 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
87 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
88 |     }
89 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
90 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
91 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
92 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
93 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
94 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
95 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
96 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
97 |     //   function f() {
98 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
99 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
100 |     //   }
101 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
102 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
103 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(root);
104 |     new PropogateConstantAnnotations(compiler, assertOnChange)
105 |         .process(externs, root);
106 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckAccidentalSemicolon.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckAccidentalSemicolon.java
Original range: 46-74
Documentation:

Method code:
46 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
47 |     Node child;
48 |     switch (n.getType()) {
49 |       case Token.IF:
50 |         child = n.getFirstChild().getNext();  // skip the condition child
51 |         break;
52 | 
53 |       case Token.WHILE:
54 |       case Token.FOR:
55 |         child = NodeUtil.getLoopCodeBlock(n);
56 |         break;
57 | 
58 |       default:
59 |         return;  // don't check other types
60 |     }
61 | 
62 |     // semicolons cause VOID children. Empty blocks are allowed because
63 |     // that's usually intentional, especially with loops.
64 |     for (; child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
65 |       if ((child.getType() == Token.BLOCK) && (!child.hasChildren())) {
66 |         // Only warn on empty blocks that replaced EMPTY nodes.  BLOCKs with no
67 |         // children are considered OK.
68 |         if (child.wasEmptyNode()) {
69 |           t.getCompiler().report(
70 |               JSError.make(t, n, level, SUSPICIOUS_SEMICOLON));
71 |         }
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 |   }

===== M0207 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerOptions.getDefineReplacements()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerOptions.java
Original range: 694-712
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns the map of define replacements.
   */
Method code:
694 |   public Map<String, Node> getDefineReplacements() {
695 |     Map<String, Node> map = Maps.newHashMap();
696 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : defineReplacements.entrySet()) {
697 |       String name = entry.getKey();
698 |       Object value = entry.getValue();
699 |       if (value instanceof Boolean) {
700 |         map.put(name, ((Boolean) value).booleanValue() ?
701 |             new Node(Token.TRUE) : new Node(Token.FALSE));
702 |       } else if (value instanceof Integer) {
703 |         map.put(name, Node.newNumber(((Integer) value).intValue()));
704 |       } else if (value instanceof Double) {
705 |         map.put(name, Node.newNumber(((Double) value).doubleValue()));
706 |       } else {
707 |         Preconditions.checkState(value instanceof String);
708 |         map.put(name, Node.newString((String) value));
709 |       }
710 |     }
711 |     return map;
712 |   }

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1330-1365
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1330 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1331 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1332 | 
1333 |     skipEOLs();
1334 | 
1335 |     boolean restArg = false;
1336 |     token = next();
1337 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1338 |       token = next();
1339 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1340 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1341 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1342 |       }
1343 |       restArg = true;
1344 |     }
1345 | 
1346 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1347 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1348 |       skipEOLs();
1349 |       if (restArg) {
1350 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1351 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1352 |         next();
1353 |         skipEOLs();
1354 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1355 |       }
1356 | 
1357 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1358 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1359 |       } else {
1360 |         next();
1361 |       }
1362 |     }
1363 | 
1364 |     return typeNode;
1365 |   }

===== M0215 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.UnreachableCodeElimination.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/UnreachableCodeElimination.java
Original range: 88-118
Documentation:

Method code:
88 |   @Override
89 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
90 |     if (parent == null) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION || n.getType() == Token.SCRIPT) {
94 |       return;
95 |     }
96 |     // Removes TRYs that had its CATCH removed and/or empty FINALLY.
97 |     if (n.getType() == Token.TRY) {
98 |       Node body = n.getFirstChild();
99 |       Node catchOrFinallyBlock = body.getNext();
100 |       Node finallyBlock = catchOrFinallyBlock.getNext();
101 | 
102 |       if (!catchOrFinallyBlock.hasChildren() &&
103 |           (finallyBlock == null || !finallyBlock.hasChildren())) {
104 |         n.removeChild(body);
105 |         parent.replaceChild(n, body);
106 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
107 |         n = body;
108 |       }
109 |     }
110 |     GraphNode<Node, Branch> gNode = curCfg.getNode(n);
111 |     if (gNode == null) { // Not in CFG.
112 |       return;
113 |     }
114 |     if (gNode.getAnnotation() != GraphReachability.REACHABLE ||
115 |         (removeNoOpStatements && !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n))) {
116 |       removeDeadExprStatementSafely(n, parent);
117 |     }
118 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal,Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 169-190
Documentation:
/**
     * If any of the variables are well-defined and alias other variables,
     * mark them as aliasing candidates.
     */
Method code:
169 |     private void collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal t,
170 |         Map<Var, ReferenceCollection> referenceMap) {
171 |       if (mode != Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
172 |         for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
173 |           Var v = it.next();
174 |           ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.get(v);
175 | 
176 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): Don't handle variables that are never used.
177 |           // The tests are much easier to write if you don't, and there's
178 |           // another pass that handles unused variables much more elegantly.
179 |           if (referenceInfo != null && referenceInfo.references.size() >= 2 &&
180 |               referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
181 |               referenceInfo.isAssignedOnce()) {
182 |             Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
183 |             Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
184 |             if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.NAME) {
185 |               aliasCandidates.put(value, new AliasCandidate(v, referenceInfo));
186 |             }
187 |           }
188 |         }
189 |       }
190 |     }

===== M0228 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 187-204
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null
191 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
192 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
193 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
194 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
196 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
197 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |   }

===== M0229 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseVariableDeclarations.GatherCollapses.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseVariableDeclarations.java
Original range: 349-378
Documentation:

Method code:
349 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
350 |       // Only care about var nodes
351 |       if (n.getType() != Token.VAR) return;
352 | 
353 |       // If we've already looked at this node, skip it
354 |       if (nodesToCollapse.contains(n)) return;
355 | 
356 |       // Adjacent VAR children of an IF node are the if and else parts and can't
357 |       // be collapsed
358 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.IF) return;
359 | 
360 |       Node varNode = n;
361 | 
362 |       // Find variable declarations that follow this one (if any)
363 |       n = n.getNext();
364 | 
365 |       boolean hasNodesToCollapse = false;
366 |       while (n != null && n.getType() == Token.VAR) {
367 |         nodesToCollapse.add(n);
368 |         hasNodesToCollapse = true;
369 | 
370 |         n = n.getNext();
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (hasNodesToCollapse) {
374 |         nodesToCollapse.add(varNode);
375 |         collapses.add(new Collapse(varNode, parent));
376 |       }
377 | 
378 |     }

===== M0240 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1500-1522
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1500 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1501 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1502 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1503 |     }
1504 | 
1505 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1506 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1507 |       next();
1508 |       skipEOLs();
1509 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1510 |       if (memberType != null) {
1511 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1512 | 
1513 |         skipEOLs();
1514 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1515 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1516 |         }
1517 | 
1518 |         next();
1519 |       }
1520 |     }
1521 |     return typeName;
1522 |   }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.newQualifiedNameNode(String,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1509-1529
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a node representing a qualified name.
   *
   * @param name A qualified name (e.g. "foo" or "foo.bar.baz")
   * @param lineno The source line offset.
   * @param charno The source character offset from start of the line.
   * @return A NAME or GETPROP node
   */
Method code:
1509 |   public static Node newQualifiedNameNode(String name, int lineno, int charno) {
1510 |     int endPos = name.indexOf('.');
1511 |     if (endPos == -1) {
1512 |       return Node.newString(Token.NAME, name, lineno, charno);
1513 |     }
1514 |     Node node = Node.newString(Token.NAME, name.substring(0, endPos),
1515 |                                lineno, charno);
1516 |     int startPos;
1517 |     do {
1518 |       startPos = endPos + 1;
1519 |       endPos = name.indexOf('.', startPos);
1520 |       String part = (endPos == -1
1521 |                      ? name.substring(startPos)
1522 |                      : name.substring(startPos, endPos));
1523 |       node = new Node(Token.GETPROP, node,
1524 |                       Node.newString(Token.STRING, part, lineno, charno),
1525 |                       lineno, charno);
1526 |     } while (endPos != -1);
1527 | 
1528 |     return node;
1529 |   }

/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
202 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
203 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
204 |     int c;
205 |     for (;;) {
206 |       c = getChar();
207 |       switch (c) {
208 |         case '*':
209 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {
210 |             addToString(c);
211 |             break;
212 |           }
213 |           // fall through
214 |         case EOF_CHAR:
215 |         case '\n':
216 |           ungetChar(c);
217 |           this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
218 |           stringBufferTop = 0;
219 |           return this.string;
220 | 
221 |         default:
222 |           addToString(c);
223 |           break;
224 |       }
225 |     }
226 |   }

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameVars.ProcessVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameVars.java
Original range: 170-234
Documentation:

Method code:
170 |     @Override
171 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
172 |       if (n.getType() != Token.NAME) {
173 |         return;
174 |       }
175 | 
176 |       String name = n.getString();
177 | 
178 |       // Ignore anonymous functions
179 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
180 |         return;
181 |       }
182 | 
183 |       // Is this local or Global?
184 |       Scope.Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name);
185 |       boolean local = (var != null) && var.isLocal();
186 | 
187 |       // Are we renaming global variables?
188 |       if (!local && localRenamingOnly) {
189 |         reservedNames.add(name);
190 |         return;
191 |       }
192 | 
193 |       // Are we renaming anonymous function names?
194 |       if (preserveAnonymousFunctionNames
195 |           && var != null
196 |           && NodeUtil.isAnonymousFunction(var.getParentNode())) {
197 |         reservedNames.add(name);
198 |         return;
199 |       }
200 | 
201 |       // Check if we can rename this.
202 |       if (!okToRenameVar(name, local)) {
203 |         if (local) {
204 |           // Blindly de-uniquify for the Prototype library for issue 103.
205 |           String newName =
206 |             MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.ContextualRenameInverter.getOrginalName(
207 |                 name);
208 |           if (!newName.equals(name)) {
209 |             n.setString(newName);
210 |           }
211 |         }
212 |         return;
213 |       }
214 | 
215 |       if (isExternsPass_) {
216 |         // Keep track of extern globals.
217 |         if (!local) {
218 |           externNames.add(name);
219 |         }
220 |         return;
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       if (local) {
224 |         // Local var: assign a new name
225 |         String tempName = LOCAL_VAR_PREFIX + var.getLocalVarIndex();
226 |         incCount(tempName, null);
227 |         localNameNodes.add(n);
228 |         localTempNames.add(tempName);
229 |       } else if (var != null) {  // Not an extern
230 |         // If it's global, increment global count
231 |         incCount(name, var.input);
232 |         globalNameNodes.add(n);
233 |       }
234 |     }

Method code:
52 |   @Override
53 |   public GraphEdge<N, E> connect(N srcValue, E edgeValue, N destValue) {
54 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> node = getDirectedGraphNode(srcValue);
55 |     if (node == null) {
56 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
57 |           srcValue + " does not exist in graph");
58 |     }
59 |     LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E> src = (LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E>) node;

62 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(

65 |     LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E> dest = (LinkedDirectedGraphNode<N, E>) node;
66 |     LinkedDirectedGraphEdge<N, E> edge =
67 |         new LinkedDirectedGraphEdge<N, E>(src, edgeValue, dest);
68 |     src.getOutEdges().add(edge);
69 |     dest.getInEdges().add(edge);
70 |     return edge;
71 |   }

===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DeadAssignmentsElimination.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DeadAssignmentsElimination.java
Original range: 57-78
Documentation:

Method code:
57 |   @Override
58 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
59 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
60 |     // Global scope _SHOULD_ work, however, liveness won't finish without
61 |     // -Xmx1024 in closure. We might have to look at coding conventions for
62 |     // exported variables as well.
63 |     if (scope.isGlobal()) {
64 |       return;
65 |     }
66 |     
67 |     // We are not going to do any dead assignment elimination in when there is
68 |     // at least one inner function because in most browsers, when there is a
69 |     // closure, ALL the variables are saved (escaped).
70 |     if (!NodeUtil.containsFunctionDeclaration(
71 |         t.getScopeRoot().getLastChild())) {
72 |       // Computes liveness information first.

/**
     * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
     * each property separated by dots.  If the node ultimately under the left
     * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
     *
     * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string

1674 |     public String getQualifiedName() {
1675 |         if (type == Token.NAME) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1225-1227
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an anonymous function? An anonymous function is one that
   * has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of anonymous functions:
   * <pre>
   * function () {}
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> anonymous:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A FUNCTION node
   * @return Whether n is an anonymous function
   */
Method code:
1225 |   static boolean isFunctionAnonymous(Node n) {
1226 |     return !isStatement(n);
1227 |   }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MinimizeExitPoints.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MinimizeExitPoints.java
Original range: 49-82
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   @Override
50 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
51 |     switch (n.getType()) {
52 |       case Token.LABEL:
53 |         tryMinimizeExits(
54 |             n.getLastChild(), Token.BREAK, n.getFirstChild().getString());
55 |         break;
56 | 
57 |       case Token.FOR:
58 |       case Token.WHILE:
59 |         tryMinimizeExits(
60 |             NodeUtil.getLoopCodeBlock(n), Token.CONTINUE, null);
61 |         break;
62 | 
63 |       case Token.DO:
64 |         tryMinimizeExits(
65 |             NodeUtil.getLoopCodeBlock(n), Token.CONTINUE, null);
66 | 
67 |         Node cond = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n);
68 |         if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(cond) && !NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(cond)) {
69 |           // Normally, we wouldn't be able to optimize BREAKs inside a loop
70 |           // but as we know the condition will always false, we can treat them
71 |           // as we would a CONTINUE.
72 |           tryMinimizeExits(
73 |               n.getFirstChild(), Token.BREAK, null);
74 |         }
75 |         break;
76 | 
77 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
78 |         tryMinimizeExits(
79 |             n.getLastChild(), Token.RETURN, null);
80 |         break;
81 |     }
82 |   }

===== M0281 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 232-260
Documentation:

Method code:
232 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
233 |     maybeEndStatement();
234 | 
235 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 | 
238 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
239 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
240 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
241 |       append(" ");
242 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
243 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
244 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
245 |       append(" ");
246 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
247 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
248 |       append(" ");
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
252 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
253 | 
254 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
255 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
256 |     // because it looks strange.
257 |     if (binOp) {
258 |       maybeCutLine();
259 |     }
260 |   }

===== M0288 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1109-1117
Documentation:
/**
     * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
     * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
     * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
     * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
     */
Method code:
1109 |     protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1110 |       if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1111 |         return -1;
1112 |       } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1113 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1114 |       } else {
1115 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1116 |       }
1117 |     }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 85-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
85 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
86 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
87 |     // just bail out early here.
88 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
89 |       return this;
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
93 | 
94 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
95 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
96 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
97 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
98 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
99 |     }
100 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
101 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
102 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
103 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
104 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
105 |         }
106 |       } else {
107 |         if (!alternate.isUnknownType()) {
108 |           Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
109 |           while (it.hasNext()) {
110 |             JSType current = it.next();
111 |             if (!current.isUnknownType()) {
112 |               if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
113 |                 // Alternate is unnecessary.
114 |                 return this;
115 |               } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
116 |                 // Alternate makes current obsolete
117 |                 it.remove();
118 |               }
119 |             }
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |         alternates.add(alternate);
123 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
124 |       }
125 |     } else {
126 |       result = null;
127 |     }
128 |     return this;
129 |   }

/**

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Denormalize.maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Denormalize.java
Original range: 65-112
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapse VARs and EXPR_RESULT node into FOR loop initializers where
   * possible.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private void maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node n, Node parent) {
66 |     // Only SCRIPT, BLOCK, and LABELs can have FORs that can be collapsed into.
67 |     // LABELs are not supported here.
68 |     if (parent == null || !NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)) {
69 |       return;
70 |     }
71 | 
72 |     // Is the current node something that can be in a for loop initializer?
73 |     if (!NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(n) && !NodeUtil.isVar(n)) {
74 |       return;
75 |     }
76 | 
77 |     // Is the next statement a valid FOR?
78 |     Node nextSibling = n.getNext();
79 |     if (nextSibling != null
80 |         && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FOR
81 |         && !NodeUtil.isForIn(nextSibling)
82 |         && nextSibling.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
83 | 
84 |       // Does the current node contain an in operator?  If so, embedding
85 |       // the expression in a for loop can cause some Javascript parsers (such
86 |       // as the Playstation 3's browser based on Access's NetFront
87 |       // browser) to fail to parse the code.
88 |       // See bug 1778863 for details.
89 |       if (NodeUtil.containsType(n, Token.IN)) {
90 |         return;
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       // Move the current node into the FOR loop initializer.
94 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
95 |       Node oldInitializer = forNode.getFirstChild();
96 |       parent.removeChild(n);
97 | 
98 |       Node newInitializer;
99 |       if (NodeUtil.isVar(n)) {
100 |         newInitializer = n;
101 |       } else {
102 |         // Extract the expression from EXPR_RESULT node.
103 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
104 |         newInitializer = n.getFirstChild();
105 |         n.removeChild(newInitializer);
106 |       }
107 | 
108 |       forNode.replaceChild(oldInitializer, newInitializer);
109 | 
110 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
111 |     }
112 |   }

/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.

Method code:
403 |   @Override
404 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
405 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
406 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
407 |     if (unknown) {
408 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
409 |       if (implicitProto == null ||

417 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.isNamespacePlaceholder(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 866-883
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is namespace placeholder.
   */
Method code:
866 |   private static boolean isNamespacePlaceholder(Node n) {
867 |     if (!n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_NAMESPACE)) {
868 |       return false;
869 |     }
870 | 
871 |     Node value = null;
872 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
873 |       Node assign = n.getFirstChild();
874 |       value = assign.getLastChild();
875 |     } else if (n.getType() == Token.VAR) {
876 |       Node name = n.getFirstChild();
877 |       value = name.getFirstChild();
878 |     }
879 | 
880 |     return value != null
881 |       && value.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT
882 |       && !value.hasChildren();
883 |   }

   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *

   *     parser may warn if another @fileoverview is found. May be null.

   *     normal jsdoc, or no jsdoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).

===== M0323 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.isBlockBoundary(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 160-190
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
160 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
161 |     if (parent != null) {
162 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
163 |         case Token.DO:
164 |         case Token.FOR:
165 |         case Token.TRY:
166 |         case Token.WHILE:
167 |         case Token.WITH:
168 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
169 |           // TRY
170 |           //   BLOCK
171 |           //   BLOCK
172 |           //     CATCH
173 |           //   BLOCK
174 |           // Note that there is an explcit CATCH token but no explicit
175 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
176 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
177 |           return true;
178 |         case Token.AND:
179 |         case Token.HOOK:
180 |         case Token.IF:
181 |         case Token.OR:
182 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
183 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
184 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
185 | 
186 |       }
187 |     }
188 | 
189 |     return n.getType() == Token.CASE;
190 |   }

===== M0343 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-139
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.CATCH:
127 |         {
128 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
129 | 
130 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
132 | 
133 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
134 |         }
135 |         break;
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     return true;
139 |   }

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 669-707
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
669 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
670 |         String name) {
671 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
672 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
673 |       // assignments.
674 | 
675 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
676 |       String prefix;
677 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
678 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
679 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
680 |       } else {
681 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
682 |         if (i == -1) {
683 |           return false;
684 |         }
685 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
686 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
687 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
688 |         while (i >= 0) {
689 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
690 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
691 |         }
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
695 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
696 |         // key, so we're done.
697 |         return true;
698 |       }
699 | 
700 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
701 |         parent = n;
702 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
703 |       }
704 | 
705 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
706 |       return true;
707 |     }

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
337 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
338 |     if (prototype == null) {
339 |       return false;
340 |     }

349 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
350 |       if (superClass != null) {

352 |       }
353 |     }
354 |     return true;

/** Attach JSDocInfo to a node, if we can find one. */

244 |   private void attachJsDoc(Comment comment, JSDocInfo info) {
245 |     Collection<NodeWithJsDoc> candidates =
246 |         nodesWithJsDoc.get(comment.getValue());
247 |     if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
248 |       return;
249 |     }

Original range: 84-118
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
84 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
85 |                            String sourceString,
86 |                            Config config,
87 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
88 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
89 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
90 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
91 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
92 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
93 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
94 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
95 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
96 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
97 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
98 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
99 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
103 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
104 |     try {
105 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
106 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
107 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
108 |     } finally {
109 |       Context.exit();
110 |     }
111 |     Node root = null;
112 |     if (astRoot != null) {
113 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
114 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
115 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
116 |     }
117 |     return root;
118 |   }

342 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
343 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

===== M0363 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 268-287
Documentation:

Method code:
268 |     @Override
269 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
270 |       switch (n.getType()) {
271 |         case Token.WHILE:
272 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
273 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
274 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
275 |             Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
276 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
277 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
278 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
279 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
280 |           }
281 |           break;
282 | 
283 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
284 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
285 |           break;
286 |       }
287 |     }

175 |     }
176 |

178 |     // It's important that they run before most checking passes.

208 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
209 |     // error off.

211 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
212 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
213 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
214 |       options.setWarningLevel(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES,
215 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
216 |     }
217 |

221 |       checks.add(checkShadowVars);
222 |     }

225 |       checks.add(checkVariableReferences);
226 |     }
227 | 
228 |     // This pass should run before types are assigned.
229 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
230 |       checks.add(objectPropertyStringPreprocess);
231 |     }
232 | 
233 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
234 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
235 |       options.checkTypes = true;
236 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
237 |       options.checkTypes = false;
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // Type-checking already does more accurate method arity checking, so don't
241 |     // do legacy method arity checking unless checkTypes is OFF.
242 |     if (options.checkTypes) {

244 |       checks.add(inferTypes.makeOneTimePass());
245 |       checks.add(checkTypes.makeOneTimePass());
246 |     } else {

248 |         checks.add(checkFunctions);
249 |       }
250 |

253 |       }

262 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.ACCESS_CONTROLS)
263 |         && options.checkTypes) {
264 |       checks.add(checkAccessControls);
265 |     }
266 |

268 |       checks.add(checkGlobalNames);
269 |     }

272 |         options.checkUnusedPropertiesEarly) {
273 |       checks.add(checkSuspiciousProperties);
274 |     }

277 |       checks.add(checkStrictMode);
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     // Defines in code always need to be processed.
281 |     checks.add(processDefines);
282 | 
283 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null ||
284 |         options.recordFunctionInformation) {
285 |       checks.add(computeFunctionNames);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(checks);
289 |     return checks;
290 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 234-326
Documentation:
/** {@inheritDoc} */
Method code:
234 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
236 |         return;
237 |       }
238 | 
239 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
240 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
241 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
242 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
243 |       }
244 | 
245 |       String name;
246 |       boolean isSet = false;
247 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
248 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
249 | 
250 |       switch (n.getType()) {
251 |         case Token.STRING:
252 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
253 |           name = null;
254 |           if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
255 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
256 |           }
257 |           if (name == null) return;
258 |           isSet = true;
259 |           type = getValueType(n.getNext());
260 |           break;
261 |         case Token.NAME:
262 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
263 |           if (parent != null) {
264 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
265 |               case Token.VAR:
266 |                 isSet = true;
267 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
268 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
269 |                 break;
270 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
271 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
272 |                   isSet = true;
273 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
274 |                 }
275 |                 break;
276 |               case Token.GETPROP:
277 |                 return;
278 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
279 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
280 |                 if (gramps == null ||
281 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(parent)) return;
282 |                 isSet = true;
283 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
284 |                 break;
285 |             }
286 |           }
287 |           name = n.getString();
288 |           break;
289 |         case Token.GETPROP:
290 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
291 |           if (parent != null) {
292 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
293 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
294 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
295 |                   isSet = true;
296 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
297 |                   isPropAssign = true;
298 |                 }
299 |                 break;
300 |               case Token.GETPROP:
301 |                 return;
302 |             }
303 |           }
304 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
305 |           if (name == null) return;
306 |           break;
307 |         default:
308 |           return;
309 |       }
310 | 
311 |       // We are only interested in global names.
312 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
313 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
314 |         return;
315 |       }
316 | 
317 |       if (isSet) {
318 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
319 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
320 |         } else {
321 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
322 |         }
323 |       } else {
324 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
325 |       }
326 |     }

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 892-912
Documentation:

Method code:
892 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
893 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
894 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
895 |       return s;
896 |     }
897 | 
898 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
899 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
900 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
901 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
902 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
903 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
904 |       // crazy.
905 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
906 |         sb.append(c);
907 |       } else {
908 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
909 |       }
910 |     }
911 |     return sb.toString();
912 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 780-807
Documentation:
/** Outputs a js string, using the optimal (single/double) quote character */
Method code:
780 |   static String jsString(String s, CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
781 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
782 | 
783 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
784 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
785 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
786 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
787 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
788 |       }
789 |     }
790 | 
791 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
792 |     char quote;
793 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
794 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
795 |       quote = '\'';
796 |       doublequote = "\"";
797 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
798 |     } else {
799 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
800 |       quote = '\"';
801 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
802 |       singlequote = "\'";
803 |     }
804 | 
805 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
806 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
807 |   }

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

/**

743 |       return null;
744 |     }

===== M0410 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldBlock(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 506-525
Documentation:
/**
   * Try removing unneeded block nodes and their useless children
   */
Method code:
506 |   void tryFoldBlock(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
507 |     // Remove any useless children
508 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; ) {
509 |       Node next = c.getNext();  // save c.next, since 'c' may be removed
510 |       if (!NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(c)) {
511 |         n.removeChild(c);  // lazy kids
512 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
513 |       }
514 |       c = next;
515 |     }
516 | 
517 |     if (n.isSyntheticBlock() || parent == null) {
518 |       return;
519 |     }
520 | 
521 |     // Try to remove the block.
522 |     if (NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(n)) {
523 |       t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
524 |     }
525 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 394-438
Documentation:

Method code:
394 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
395 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
396 |       cursor++;
397 |       --ungetCursor;
398 |       if (charno == -1) {
399 |         charno = getOffset();
400 |       }
401 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     for(;;) {
405 |       int c;
406 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
407 |         hitEOF = true;
408 |         if (charno == -1) {
409 |           charno = getOffset();
410 |         }
411 |         return EOF_CHAR;
412 |       }
413 |       cursor++;
414 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
415 | 
416 | 
417 |       if (c <= 127) {
418 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
419 |           lineEndChar = c;
420 |           c = '\n';
421 |         }
422 |       } else {
423 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
424 |           continue;
425 |         }
426 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
427 |           lineEndChar = c;
428 |           c = '\n';
429 |         }
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       if (charno == -1) {
433 |         charno = getOffset();
434 |       }
435 | 
436 |       return c;
437 |     }
438 |   }

===== M0419 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 86-624
Documentation:

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
100 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
101 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
102 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
114 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
115 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else {
118 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
119 |       }
120 |       return;
121 |     }
122 | 
123 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
124 | 
125 |     switch (type) {
126 |       case Token.TRY: {
127 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
128 |                 first.getNext().getChildCount() <= 1);
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
130 | 
131 |         add("try");
132 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
133 | 
134 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
135 |         // isn't a catch block
136 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
137 |         if (catchblock != null) {
138 |           add(catchblock);
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         if (childCount == 3) {
142 |           add("finally");
143 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
144 |         }
145 |         break;
146 |       }
147 | 
148 |       case Token.CATCH:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
150 |         if (first.getNext().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
151 |           throw new Error("Catch conditions not suppored because I think" +
152 |                           " that it may be a netscape only feature.");
153 |         }
154 | 
155 |         add("catch(");
156 |         add(first);
157 |         add(")");
158 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
159 |         break;
160 | 
161 |       case Token.THROW:
162 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
163 |         add("throw");
164 |         add(first);
165 | 
166 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
167 |         // parse this.
168 |         cc.endStatement(true);
169 |         break;
170 | 
171 |       case Token.RETURN:
172 |         add("return");
173 |         if (childCount == 1) {
174 |           add(first);
175 |         } else {
176 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
177 |         }
178 |         cc.endStatement();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.VAR:
182 |         if (first != null) {
183 |           add("var ");
184 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
185 |         }
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.NAME:
189 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
190 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
191 |         } else {
192 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
195 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
196 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
197 |           } else {
198 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
199 |             // precedence.
200 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
201 |           }
202 |         }
203 | 
204 |         break;
205 | 
206 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
207 |         add("[");
208 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
209 |         add("]");
210 |         break;
211 | 
212 |       case Token.LP:
213 |         add("(");
214 |         addList(first);
215 |         add(")");
216 |         break;
217 | 
218 |       case Token.COMMA:
219 |         addList(first, false, context);
220 |         break;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.NUMBER:
223 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
224 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
228 |       case Token.VOID:
229 |       case Token.NOT:
230 |       case Token.BITNOT:
231 |       case Token.POS:
232 |       case Token.NEG: {
233 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
234 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
235 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
236 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
237 |         break;
238 |       }
239 | 
240 |       case Token.HOOK: {
241 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
242 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
243 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
244 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
245 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), p);
246 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
247 |         addExpr(last, p);
248 |         break;
249 |       }
250 | 
251 |       case Token.REGEXP:
252 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
253 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
254 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
255 |         }
256 | 
257 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
258 | 
259 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
260 |         if (childCount == 2) {
261 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
262 |         } else {
263 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
264 |           add(regexp);
265 |         }
266 |         break;
267 | 
268 |       case Token.GET_REF:
269 |         add(first);
270 |         break;
271 | 
272 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
273 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
274 |         add(first);
275 |         add(".");
276 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
277 |         break;
278 | 
279 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
280 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
281 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
282 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
283 |           add("(");
284 |         }
285 | 
286 |         add("function");
287 |         add(first);
288 | 
289 |         add(first.getNext());
290 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
291 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
292 | 
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add(")");
295 |         }
296 |         break;
297 | 
298 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
299 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
300 |         boolean stripBlock = n.isSyntheticBlock() ||
301 |             ((context != Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK) && (n.getChildCount() < 2));
302 |         if (!stripBlock) {
303 |           cc.beginBlock();
304 |         }
305 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
306 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
307 | 
308 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
309 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
310 |             cc.endStatement();
311 |           }
312 | 
313 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
314 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
315 |           }
316 | 
317 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
318 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
319 |           if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
320 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
321 |           }
322 |         }
323 |         if (!stripBlock) {
324 |           cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
325 |         }
326 |         break;
327 |       }
328 | 
329 |       case Token.FOR:
330 |         if (childCount == 4) {
331 |           add("for(");
332 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
333 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
334 |           } else {
335 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
336 |           }
337 |           add(";");
338 |           add(first.getNext());
339 |           add(";");
340 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
341 |           add(")");
342 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
343 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
344 |         } else {
345 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
346 |           add("for(");
347 |           add(first);
348 |           add("in");
349 |           add(first.getNext());
350 |           add(")");
351 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
352 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
353 |         }
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.DO:
357 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
358 |         add("do");
359 |         addNonEmptyExpression(first, Context.OTHER, false);
360 |         add("while(");
361 |         add(last);
362 |         add(")");
363 |         cc.endStatement();
364 |         break;
365 | 
366 |       case Token.WHILE:
367 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
368 |         add("while(");
369 |         add(first);
370 |         add(")");
371 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
372 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
373 |         break;
374 | 
375 |       case Token.EMPTY:
376 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
380 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
381 |         Preconditions.checkState(last.getType() == Token.STRING);
382 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
383 |         if (needsParens) {
384 |           add("(");
385 |         }
386 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
387 |         if (needsParens) {
388 |           add(")");
389 |         }
390 |         add(".");
391 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
392 |         break;
393 |       }
394 | 
395 |       case Token.GETELEM:
396 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
397 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
398 |         add("[");
399 |         add(first.getNext());
400 |         add("]");
401 |         break;
402 | 
403 |       case Token.WITH:
404 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
405 |         add("with(");
406 |         add(first);
407 |         add(")");
408 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
409 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
410 |         break;
411 | 
412 |       case Token.INC:
413 |       case Token.DEC: {
414 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
415 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
416 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
417 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
418 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
419 |         if (postProp != 0) {
420 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
421 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
422 |         } else {
423 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
424 |           add(first);
425 |         }
426 |         break;
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       case Token.CALL:
430 |         // If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
431 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
432 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
433 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
434 |         if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
435 |             "eval".equals(first.getString()) &&
436 |             !first.getBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL)) {
437 |           add("(0,eval)");
438 |         } else {
439 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
440 |         }
441 |         add("(");
442 |         addList(first.getNext());
443 |         add(")");
444 |         break;
445 | 
446 |       case Token.IF:
447 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
448 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
449 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
450 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
451 |           cc.beginBlock();
452 |         }
453 | 
454 |         add("if(");
455 |         add(first);
456 |         add(")");
457 | 
458 |         if (hasElse) {
459 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
460 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
461 |           add("else");
462 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
463 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
464 |         } else {
465 |           addNonEmptyExpression(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
466 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
467 |         }
468 | 
469 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
470 |           cc.endBlock();
471 |         }
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.NULL:
475 |       case Token.THIS:
476 |       case Token.FALSE:
477 |       case Token.TRUE:
478 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
479 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
480 |         break;
481 | 
482 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
484 |         add("continue");
485 |         if (childCount == 1) {
486 |           add(" ");
487 |           add(first);
488 |         }
489 |         cc.endStatement();
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
493 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
494 |         add("debugger");
495 |         cc.endStatement();
496 |         break;
497 | 
498 |       case Token.BREAK:
499 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
500 |         add("break");
501 |         if (childCount == 1) {
502 |           add(" ");
503 |           add(first);
504 |         }
505 |         cc.endStatement();
506 |         break;
507 | 
508 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
509 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
510 |         if (type == Token.EXPR_VOID && validation) {
511 |           throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
512 |         }
513 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
514 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
515 |         cc.endStatement();
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.NEW:
519 |         add("new ");
520 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
521 | 
522 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
523 |         // to force parens. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
524 |         // first viable parens
525 |         if (NodeUtil.containsCall(first)) {
526 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
527 |         }
528 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
529 | 
530 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
531 |         Node next = first.getNext();
532 |         if (next != null) {
533 |           add("(");
534 |           addList(next);
535 |           add(")");
536 |         }
537 |         break;
538 | 
539 |       case Token.STRING:
540 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
541 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
542 |         break;
543 | 
544 |       case Token.DELPROP:
545 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
546 |         add("delete ");
547 |         add(first);
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
551 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount % 2 == 0);
552 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
553 |         if (needsParens) {
554 |           add("(");
555 |         }
556 |         add("{");
557 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext().getNext()) {
558 |           if (c != first) {
559 |             cc.listSeparator();
560 |           }
561 | 
562 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
563 |           // not JavaScript keywords
564 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
565 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
566 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
567 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
568 |               // unicode escaped.
569 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
570 |             add(c.getString());
571 |           } else {
572 |             addExpr(c, 1);
573 |           }
574 |           add(":");
575 |           addExpr(c.getNext(), 1);
576 |         }
577 |         add("}");
578 |         if (needsParens) {
579 |           add(")");
580 |         }
581 |         break;
582 |       }
583 | 
584 |       case Token.SWITCH:
585 |         add("switch(");
586 |         add(first);
587 |         add(")");
588 |         cc.beginBlock();
589 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
590 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
591 |         break;
592 | 
593 |       case Token.CASE:
594 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
595 |         add("case ");
596 |         add(first);
597 |         addCaseBody(last);
598 |         break;
599 | 
600 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
601 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
602 |         add("default");
603 |         addCaseBody(first);
604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.LABEL:
607 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
608 |         add(first);
609 |         add(":");
610 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
611 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
612 |         break;
613 | 
614 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
615 |       // ignored for our purposes.
616 |       case Token.SETNAME:
617 |         break;
618 | 
619 |       default:
620 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
624 |   }

===== M0426 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 205-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * function() { return a; } is not.
   */
Method code:
205 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n) {
206 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Refine this function to catch more literals.
207 |     switch (n.getType()) {
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
210 |       case Token.REGEXP:
211 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
212 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
213 |              child = child.getNext()) {
214 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child)) {
215 |             return false;
216 |           }
217 |         }
218 |         return true;
219 | 
220 |       default:
221 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
222 |     }
223 |   }

===== M0433 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 369-384
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
369 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
370 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
371 |       Set<String> directives = null;
372 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
373 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
374 |         if (directives == null) {
375 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
376 |         } else {
377 |           directives.add(directive);
378 |         }
379 |       }
380 | 
381 |       if (directives != null) {
382 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
383 |       }
384 |     }

===== M0434 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 145-186
Documentation:

Method code:
145 |   @Override
146 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
147 |     this.root = root;
148 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
149 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
150 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
151 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities);
152 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
153 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
154 | 
155 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
156 |     // search on the CFG.
157 |     priorityCounter = 0;
158 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
159 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
160 | 
161 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
162 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
163 |       // priority of them too.
164 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
165 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
166 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
167 |           Preconditions.checkState(
168 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
169 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
170 |         }
171 |       }
172 |     }
173 | 
174 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
175 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
176 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
177 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
178 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
179 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
180 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
181 |       }
182 |     }
183 | 
184 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
185 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
186 |   }

===== M0436 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1350-1375
Documentation:
/**
     * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair
     * of nodes that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal.
     * Package private for testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
     */
Method code:
1350 |     NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1351 |         boolean eq = false;
1352 | 
1353 |         if (type == node2.getType() &&
1354 |             getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount() &&
1355 |             getClass() == node2.getClass()) {
1356 | 
1357 |             eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1358 |         }
1359 | 
1360 |         if (!eq) {
1361 |             return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1362 |         }
1363 | 
1364 |         NodeMismatch res = null;
1365 |         Node n, n2;
1366 |         for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1367 |              res == null && n != null;
1368 |              n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1369 |             res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1370 |             if (res != null) {
1371 |               return res;
1372 |             }
1373 |         }
1374 |         return res;
1375 |     }
