===== M0441 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessClosurePrimitives.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessClosurePrimitives.java
Original range: 152-236
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   */
Method code:
152 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
153 |     switch (n.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.CALL:
155 |         boolean isExpr = parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT;
156 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
157 |         if (left.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
158 |           Node name = left.getFirstChild();
159 |           if (name.getType() == Token.NAME &&
160 |               GOOG.equals(name.getString())) {
161 |             // For the sake of simplicity, we report code changes
162 |             // when we see a provides/requires, and don't worry about
163 |             // reporting the change when we actually do the replacement.
164 |             String methodName = name.getNext().getString();
165 |             if ("base".equals(methodName)) {
166 |               processBaseClassCall(t, n);
167 |             } else if (!isExpr) {
168 |               // All other methods must be called in an EXPR.
169 |               break;
170 |             } else if ("require".equals(methodName)) {
171 |               processRequireCall(t, n, parent);
172 |             } else if ("provide".equals(methodName)) {
173 |               processProvideCall(t, n, parent);
174 |             } else if ("exportSymbol".equals(methodName)) {
175 |               Node arg = left.getNext();
176 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.STRING) {
177 |                 int dot = arg.getString().indexOf('.');
178 |                 if (dot == -1) {
179 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString());
180 |                 } else {
181 |                   exportedVariables.add(arg.getString().substring(0, dot));
182 |                 }
183 |               }
184 |             } else if ("addDependency".equals(methodName)) {
185 |               CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
186 |               List<String> typeDecls =
187 |                   convention.identifyTypeDeclarationCall(n);
188 |               if (typeDecls != null) {
189 |                 for (String typeDecl : typeDecls) {
190 |                   compiler.getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(typeDecl);
191 |                 }
192 |               }
193 | 
194 |               // We can't modify parent, so just create a node that will
195 |               // get compiled out.
196 |               parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(0));
197 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
198 |             } else if ("setCssNameMapping".equals(methodName)) {
199 |               processSetCssNameMapping(t, n, parent);
200 |             }
201 |           }
202 |         }
203 |         break;
204 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
205 |       case Token.NAME:
206 |         // If this is an assignment to a provided name, remove the provided
207 |         // object.
208 |         handleCandidateProvideDefinition(t, n, parent);
209 |         break;
210 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
211 |         // If this is a declaration of a provided named function, this is an
212 |         // error. Hosited functions will explode if the're provided.
213 |         if (t.inGlobalScope() &&
214 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n)) {
215 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
216 |           ProvidedName pn = providedNames.get(name);
217 |           if (pn != null) {
218 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, FUNCTION_NAMESPACE_ERROR, name));
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |         break;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.NEW:
224 |         trySimplifyNewDate(t, n, parent);
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.GETPROP:
228 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME &&
229 |             parent.getType() != Token.CALL &&
230 |             parent.getType() != Token.ASSIGN &&
231 |             "goog.base".equals(n.getQualifiedName())) {
232 |           reportBadBaseClassUse(t, n, "May only be called directly.");
233 |         }
234 |         break;
235 |     }
236 |   }

/**

   * Covert EXPR_VOID to EXPR_RESULT to simplify the rest of the code.

71 |   private void normalizeNodeTypes(Node n) {
72 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EXPR_VOID) {

75 |     }

78 |     // produced by the two parsers.

88 |       // parent pointer check serves as a more general check.

Original range: 541-580

Method code:
541 |   private void compileInternal() {
542 |     parse();
543 |     if (hasErrors()) {
544 |       return;
545 |     }
546 | 
547 |     if (!precheck()) {
548 |       return;
549 |     }
550 | 
551 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
552 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
553 |       check();
554 |       return;
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
558 |       check();
559 |       if (hasErrors()) {
560 |         return;
561 |       }
562 | 
563 |       if (options.externExportsPath != null) {
564 |         externExports();
565 |       }
566 | 
567 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
568 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
569 |         optimize();
570 |       }
571 |     }
572 | 
573 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
574 |       recordFunctionInformation();
575 |     }
576 | 
577 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
578 |       runSanityCheck();
579 |     }
580 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 136-163
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
136 |   JSType build() {
137 |     if (result == null) {
138 |       if (isAllType) {
139 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
140 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
141 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
142 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
143 |         } else {
144 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
145 |         }
146 |       } else {
147 |         Set<JSType> alternateSet = Sets.newUnmodifiableHashSet(alternates);
148 |         int size = alternateSet.size();
149 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
150 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
151 |         } else {
152 |           if (size > 1) {
153 |             result = new UnionType(registry, alternateSet);
154 |           } else if (size == 1) {
155 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
156 |           } else {
157 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
158 |           }
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 |     }
162 |     return result;
163 |   }

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 89-170
Documentation:
/** {@inheritDoc} */
Method code:
89 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
90 |     if (n.getType() != Token.NAME) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     if (NodeUtil.isLabelName(n)) {
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     String varName = n.getString();
99 | 
100 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
101 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
102 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunction(parent));
103 | 
104 |       // A function declaration with an empty name passes Rhino,
105 |       // but is supposed to be a syntax error according to the spec.
106 |       if (!NodeUtil.isAnonymousFunction(parent)) {
107 |         t.report(n, INVALID_FUNCTION_DECL);
108 |       }
109 |       return;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
113 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
114 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
115 |     if (var == null) {
116 |       if (NodeUtil.isAnonymousFunction(parent)) {
117 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
118 |         // current scope.
119 |       } else {
120 |         t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
121 | 
122 |         if (sanityCheck) {
123 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
124 |         } else {
125 |           // Create a new variable in a synthetic script. This will prevent
126 |           // subsequent compiler passes from crashing.
127 |           Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, varName);
128 |           getSynthesizedExternsRoot().addChildToBack(
129 |               new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode));
130 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, nameNode,
131 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
132 |         }
133 |       }
134 |       return;
135 |     }
136 | 
137 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
138 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
139 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
140 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
141 |       return;
142 |     }
143 | 
144 |     // Check module dependencies.
145 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
146 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
147 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
148 |     if (varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
149 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
150 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
151 |       } else {
152 |         if (!sanityCheck && scope.isGlobal()) {
153 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
154 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
155 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
156 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
157 |           } else {
158 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
159 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
160 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
161 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
162 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
163 |           }
164 |         } else {
165 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
166 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
167 |         }
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 976-1050
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
976 |   Node parseInputs() {
977 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
978 | 
979 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
980 |     // individual file parse trees.
981 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
982 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
983 |     }
984 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
985 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
986 |     }
987 | 
988 |     // Parse main js sources.
989 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
990 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
991 | 
992 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
993 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
994 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
995 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
999 | 
1000 |     try {
1001 |       // Parse externs sources.
1002 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1003 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1004 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1005 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1006 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1007 |           return null;
1008 |         }
1009 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1010 |       }
1011 | 
1012 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1013 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1014 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1015 |           return null;
1016 |         }
1017 | 
1018 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1019 |         if (n == null) {
1020 |           continue;
1021 |         }
1022 | 
1023 |         if (devMode) {
1024 |           runSanityCheck();
1025 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1026 |             return null;
1027 |           }
1028 |         }
1029 | 
1030 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1031 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1032 | 
1033 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1034 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1035 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1036 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(input.getName());
1037 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1038 |         }
1039 | 
1040 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1041 |       }
1042 | 
1043 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1044 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1045 | 
1046 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1047 |     } finally {
1048 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1049 |     }
1050 |   }

===== M0476 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 360-404
Documentation:

Method code:
360 |   @Override
361 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
362 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
363 |       return true;
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     // Union types
367 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
368 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
369 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
370 |       return false;
371 |     }
372 | 
373 |     // record types
374 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
375 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
376 |     }
377 | 
378 |     // Interfaces
379 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
380 |     // to the interface instance.
381 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
382 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
383 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
384 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
385 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
386 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
387 |           return true;
388 |         }
389 |       }
390 |     }
391 | 
392 |     // other prototype based objects
393 |     if (that != null) {
394 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
395 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
396 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
397 |         // to avoid guessing.
398 |         return true;
399 |       }
400 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     return false;
404 |   }

===== M0500 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 524-531
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
524 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, ObjectType owner) {
525 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
526 |     if (typeSet == null) {
527 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
528 |     }
529 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
530 |     typeSet.add(owner);
531 |   }

304 | 
305 |     if (!options.idGenerators.isEmpty()) {
306 |       passes.add(replaceIdGenerators);
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     // Optimizes references to the arguments variable.
310 |     if (options.optimizeArgumentsArray) {
311 |       passes.add(optimizeArgumentsArray);
312 |     }
313 | 
314 |     // Remove all parameters that are constants or unused.
315 |     if (options.optimizeParameters) {
316 |       passes.add(removeUselessParameters);
317 |     }
318 | 
319 |     // Abstract method removal works best on minimally modified code, and also
320 |     // only needs to run once.
321 |     if (options.closurePass && options.removeAbstractMethods) {
322 |       passes.add(removeAbstractMethods);
323 |     }
324 | 
325 |     // Collapsing properties can undo constant inlining, so we do this before
326 |     // the main optimization loop.
327 |     if (options.collapseProperties) {
328 |       passes.add(collapseProperties);
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     // Tighten types based on actual usage.
332 |     if (options.tightenTypes) {
333 |       passes.add(tightenTypesBuilder);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     // Property disambiguation should only run once and needs to be done
337 |     // soon after type checking, both so that it can make use of type
338 |     // information and so that other passes can take advantage of the renamed
339 |     // properties.
340 |     if (options.disambiguateProperties) {
341 |       passes.add(disambiguateProperties);
342 |     }
343 | 
344 |     if (options.computeFunctionSideEffects) {
345 |       passes.add(markPureFunctions);
346 |     } else if (options.markNoSideEffectCalls) {
347 |       // TODO(user) The properties that this pass adds to CALL and NEW
348 |       // AST nodes increase the AST's in-memory size.  Given that we are
349 |       // already running close to our memory limits, we could run into
350 |       // trouble if we end up using the @nosideeffects annotation a lot
351 |       // or compute @nosideeffects annotations by looking at function
352 |       // bodies.  It should be easy to propagate @nosideeffects
353 |       // annotations as part of passes that depend on this property and
354 |       // store the result outside the AST (which would allow garbage
355 |       // collection once the pass is done).
356 |       passes.add(markNoSideEffectCalls);
357 |     }
358 | 
359 |     if (options.chainCalls) {
360 |       passes.add(chainCalls);
361 |     }
362 | 
363 |     // Constant checking must be done after property collapsing because
364 |     // property collapsing can introduce new constants (e.g. enum values).
365 |     if (options.inlineConstantVars) {
366 |       passes.add(checkConsts);
367 |     }
368 | 
369 |     // The Caja library adds properties to Object.prototype, which breaks
370 |     // most for-in loops.  This adds a check to each loop that skips
371 |     // any property matching /___$/.
372 |     if (options.ignoreCajaProperties) {
373 |       passes.add(ignoreCajaProperties);
374 |     }
375 | 
376 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(passes);
377 | 
378 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval || options.reportPath != null) {
379 |       passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
380 |       passes.add(smartNamePass);
381 |     }
382 | 
383 |     // TODO(user): This forces a first crack at crossModuleCodeMotion
384 |     // before devirtualization. Once certain functions are devirtualized,
385 |     // it confuses crossModuleCodeMotion ability to recognized that
386 |     // it is recursive.
387 | 
388 |     // TODO(user): This is meant for a temporary quick win.
389 |     // In the future, we might want to improve our analysis in
390 |     // CrossModuleCodeMotion so we don't need to do this.
391 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
392 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     // Method devirtualization benefits from property disambiguiation so
396 |     // it should run after that pass but before passes that do
397 |     // optimizations based on global names (like cross module code motion
398 |     // and inline functions).  Smart Name Removal does better if run before
399 |     // this pass.
400 |     if (options.devirtualizePrototypeMethods) {
401 |       passes.add(devirtualizePrototypeMethods);
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
405 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
406 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.BEFORE_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
407 |     }
408 | 
409 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeMainOptimizations"));
410 | 
411 |     passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
412 | 
413 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeModuleMotion"));
414 | 
415 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
416 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
420 |       passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
421 |     }
422 | 
423 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("afterModuleMotion"));
424 | 
425 |     // Some optimizations belong outside the loop because running them more
426 |     // than once would either have no benefit or be incorrect.
427 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
428 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
429 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.AFTER_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     if (options.flowSensitiveInlineVariables) {
433 |       passes.add(flowSensitiveInlineVariables);
434 | 
435 |       // After inlining some of the variable uses, some variables are unused.
436 |       // Re-run remove unused vars to clean it up.
437 |       if (options.removeUnusedVars) {
438 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
439 |       }
440 |     }
441 | 
442 |     if (options.collapseAnonymousFunctions) {
443 |       passes.add(collapseAnonymousFunctions);
444 |     }
445 | 
446 |     // Move functions before extracting prototype member declarations.
447 |     if (options.moveFunctionDeclarations) {
448 |       passes.add(moveFunctionDeclarations);
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
452 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.MAPPED) {
453 |       passes.add(nameMappedAnonymousFunctions);
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // The mapped name anonymous function pass makes use of information that
457 |     // the extract prototype member declarations pass removes so the former
458 |     // happens before the latter.
459 |     //
460 |     // Extracting prototype properties screws up the heuristic renaming
461 |     // policies, so never run it when those policies are requested.
462 |     if (options.extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations &&
463 |         (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.HEURISTIC &&
464 |          options.propertyRenaming !=
465 |             PropertyRenamingPolicy.AGGRESSIVE_HEURISTIC)) {
466 |       passes.add(extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations);
467 |     }
468 | 
469 |     if (options.coalesceVariableNames) {
470 |       passes.add(coalesceVariableNames);
471 |     }
472 | 
473 |     if (options.ambiguateProperties &&
474 |         (options.propertyRenaming == PropertyRenamingPolicy.ALL_UNQUOTED)) {
475 |       passes.add(ambiguateProperties);
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     if (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
479 |       passes.add(renameProperties);
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     // Reserve global names added to the "windows" object.
483 |     if (options.reserveRawExports) {
484 |       passes.add(gatherRawExports);
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     // This comes after property renaming because quoted property names must
488 |     // not be renamed.
489 |     if (options.convertToDottedProperties) {
490 |       passes.add(convertToDottedProperties);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     // Property renaming must happen before this pass runs since this
494 |     // pass may convert dotted properties into quoted properties.  It
495 |     // is beneficial to run before alias strings, alias keywords and
496 |     // variable renaming.
497 |     if (options.rewriteFunctionExpressions) {
498 |       passes.add(rewriteFunctionExpressions);
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     // This comes after converting quoted property accesses to dotted property
502 |     // accesses in order to avoid aliasing property names.
503 |     if (!options.aliasableStrings.isEmpty() || options.aliasAllStrings) {
504 |       passes.add(aliasStrings);
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     if (options.aliasExternals) {
508 |       passes.add(aliasExternals);
509 |     }
510 | 
511 |     if (options.aliasKeywords) {
512 |       passes.add(aliasKeywords);
513 |     }
514 | 
515 |     if (options.collapseVariableDeclarations) {
516 |       passes.add(collapseVariableDeclarations);
517 |     }
518 | 
519 |     passes.add(denormalize);
520 | 
521 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null) {
522 |       passes.add(instrumentFunctions);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.ALL) {
526 |       // If we're leaving some (or all) variables with their old names,
527 |       // then we need to undo any of the markers we added for distinguishing
528 |       // local variables ("$$1").
529 |       passes.add(invertContextualRenaming);
530 |     }
531 | 
532 | 
533 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
534 |       passes.add(renameVars);
535 |     }
536 | 
537 |     // This pass should run after names stop changing.
538 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
539 |       passes.add(objectPropertyStringPostprocess);
540 |     }
541 | 
542 |     if (options.labelRenaming) {
543 |       passes.add(renameLabels);
544 |     }
545 | 
546 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
547 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.UNMAPPED) {
548 |       passes.add(nameUnmappedAnonymousFunctions);
549 |     }
550 | 
551 |     // Safety check
552 |     if (options.checkSymbols) {
553 |       passes.add(sanityCheckVars);
554 |     }
555 | 
556 |     return passes;
557 |   }

/**
     * Inform the compiler of all the changes this result object had recorded.
     * It might trigger a {@link Compiler#recentChange}, re-throw all the

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 770-784
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
770 |   private static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
771 |     switch (n.getType()) {
772 |       case Token.DO:
773 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
774 |       case Token.FOR:
775 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
776 |           return n;
777 |         }
778 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
779 |       case Token.LABEL:
780 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
781 |       default:
782 |         return n;
783 |     }
784 |   }

===== M0513 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameVars.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameVars.java
Original range: 278-331

/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   */
Method code:
278 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
279 |     assignmentLog = new StringBuilder();
280 | 
281 |     // Do variable reference counting.
282 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, externs, new ProcessVars(true));
283 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new ProcessVars(false));
284 | 
285 |     // Make sure that new names don't overlap with extern names.
286 |     reservedNames.addAll(externNames);
287 | 
288 |     // Rename vars, sorted by frequency of occurrence to minimize code size.
289 |     SortedSet<Assignment> varsByFrequency =
290 |         new TreeSet<Assignment>(FREQUENCY_COMPARATOR);
291 |     varsByFrequency.addAll(assignments.values());
292 | 
293 |     // First try to reuse names from an earlier compilation.
294 |     if (prevUsedRenameMap != null) {
295 |       reusePreviouslyUsedVariableMap();
296 |     }
297 | 
298 |     // Assign names, sorted by descending frequency to minimize code size.
299 |     assignNames(varsByFrequency);
300 | 
301 |     boolean changed = false;
302 | 
303 |     // Rename the globals!
304 |     for (Node n : globalNameNodes) {
305 |       String newName = getNewGlobalName(n);
306 |       // Note: if newName is null, then oldName is an extern.
307 |       if (newName != null) {
308 |         n.setString(newName);
309 |         changed = true;
310 |       }
311 |     }
312 | 
313 |     // Rename the locals!
314 |     int count = 0;
315 |     for (Node n : localNameNodes) {
316 |       String newName = getNewLocalName(n, count);
317 |       if (newName != null) {
318 |         n.setString(newName);
319 |         changed = true;
320 |       }
321 |       count++;
322 |     }
323 | 
324 |     if (changed) {
325 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
326 |     }
327 | 
328 |     // Lastly, write the name assignments to the debug log.
329 |     compiler.addToDebugLog("JS var assignments:\n" + assignmentLog);
330 |     assignmentLog = null;
331 |   }

===== M0517 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 670-763
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
670 |   private Node computeFollowNode(Node fromNode, Node node) {
671 |     /*
672 |      * This is the case where:
673 |      *
674 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
675 |      * the script.
676 |      *
677 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
678 |      * the caller of the function.
679 |      *
680 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
681 |      * back to the caller of the function.
682 |      *
683 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
684 |      * asked to traverse.
685 |      *
686 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
687 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
688 |      */
689 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
690 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION || node == root) {
691 |       return null;
692 |     }
693 | 
694 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
695 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
696 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
697 |       case Token.IF:
698 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
699 |       case Token.CASE:
700 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
701 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
702 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
703 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
704 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
705 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
706 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
707 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
708 |           } else {
709 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
710 |           }
711 |         } else {
712 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
713 |         }
714 |         break;
715 |       case Token.FOR:
716 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
717 |           return parent;
718 |         } else {
719 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
720 |         }
721 |       case Token.WHILE:
722 |       case Token.DO:
723 |         return parent;
724 |       case Token.TRY:
725 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
726 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
727 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
728 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
729 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
730 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
731 |           }
732 |         // CATCH block.
733 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
734 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
735 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
736 |           } else {
737 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
738 |           }
739 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
740 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
741 |           for (Node finallyNode : finallyMap.get(parent)) {
742 |             createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
743 |           }
744 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
745 |         }
746 |     }
747 | 
748 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
749 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
750 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
751 | 
752 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
753 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
754 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
755 |     }
756 | 
757 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
758 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
759 |     } else {
760 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
761 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent);
762 |     }
763 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
357 |   private CompilerInput[] getAllInputsFromModules() {
358 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = new ArrayList<CompilerInput>();
359 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = new HashMap<String, JSModule>();
360 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
361 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
362 |         String inputName = input.getName();
363 |         JSModule firstModule = inputMap.get(inputName);
364 |         if (firstModule == null) {
365 |           inputs.add(input);
366 |           inputMap.put(inputName, module);
367 |         } else {
368 |           report(JSError.make(DUPLICATE_INPUT_IN_MODULES,
369 |               firstModule.getName(), module.getName(), inputName));
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 |     }
373 |     if (hasErrors()) {
374 | 
375 |       // There's no reason to bother parsing the code.
376 |       return new CompilerInput[0];
377 |     }
378 | 
379 |     return inputs.toArray(new CompilerInput[inputs.size()]);
380 |   }

   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression

   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'

Method code:
1443 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1444 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1445 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));

===== M0527 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 477-500
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
477 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |       JSDocInfo info;
479 |       int valueNodeType;
480 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
481 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
482 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
483 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
484 |           break;
485 |         case Token.VAR:
486 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
487 |           if (info == null) {
488 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
489 |           }
490 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
491 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
492 |           break;
493 |         default:
494 |           return false;
495 |       }
496 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
497 |       return info != null &&
498 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
499 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
500 |     }

286 |       @Override
287 |       public int parseArguments(Parameters params) throws CmdLineException {
288 |         String param = params.getParameter(0);
289 |         if (param == null) {
290 |           setter.addValue(true);
291 |           return 0;

/**
   * Provides the array of available characters based on the specified arrays.
   * @param chars The list of characters that are legal
   * @param reservedCharacters The characters that should not be used
   * @return An array of characters to use. Will return the chars array if
   *    reservedCharacters is null or empty, otherwise creates a new array.
   */
Method code:
79 |   static char[] reserveCharacters(char[] chars, char[] reservedCharacters) {
80 |     if (reservedCharacters == null || reservedCharacters.length == 0) {
81 |       return chars;
82 |     }    
83 |     Set<Character> charSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet(Chars.asList(chars));
84 |     for (char reservedCharacter : reservedCharacters) {
85 |       charSet.remove(reservedCharacter);
86 |     }
87 |     return Chars.toArray(charSet);
88 |   }

===== M0542 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 176-197
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
176 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
177 |     switch (n.getType()) {
178 |       case Token.STRING:
179 |       case Token.NUMBER:
180 |       case Token.NULL:
181 |       case Token.TRUE:
182 |       case Token.FALSE:
183 |       case Token.VOID:
184 |         return true;
185 |       case Token.NEG:
186 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         String name = n.getString();
189 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
190 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
191 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
192 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
193 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     return false;
197 |   }

/**
     * Try to collapse the given assign into subsequent expressions.
     */
Method code:
127 |     private void collapseAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign, Node expr,
128 |         Node exprParent) {
129 |       Node leftValue = assign.getFirstChild();
130 |       Node rightValue = leftValue.getNext();
131 |       if (isCollapsibleValue(leftValue, true) &&
132 |           collapseAssignEqualTo(expr, exprParent, leftValue)) {
133 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
134 |       } else if (isCollapsibleValue(rightValue, false) &&
135 |           collapseAssignEqualTo(expr, exprParent, rightValue)) {
136 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
137 |       } else if (rightValue.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
138 |         // Recursively deal with nested assigns.
139 |         collapseAssign(t, rightValue, expr, exprParent);
140 |       }
141 |     }

===== M0546 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RemoveUnusedVars.traverseNode(Node,Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RemoveUnusedVars.java
Original range: 115-150
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses everything in the current scope and marks variables that
   * are referenced. Functions create their own scope, so we don't
   * recurse into them unless they are called.
   */
Method code:
115 |   private void traverseNode(Node n, Node parent, Scope scope) {
116 |     int type = n.getType();
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       // We traverse a function only if the function definition is
119 |       // immediately being referenced, e.g.
120 |       // - return function() { ... };
121 |       // - foo( function() { ... } )
122 |       // - array[ function() { ... } ];
123 |       //
124 |       // Otherwise we traverse into the function only when we encounter
125 |       // a reference to it (see markReferencedVar())
126 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
127 |         // If it's an exported function, or an anonymous function, assume
128 |         // that it'll be called.
129 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n) ||
130 |             compiler_.getCodingConvention().isExported(
131 |                 n.getFirstChild().getString())) {
132 |           traverseFunction(n, scope);
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.NAME:
137 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR) {
138 |           // All non-var declarations are references to other vars
139 |           Var var = scope.getVar(n.getString());
140 |           if (var != null) {
141 |             markReferencedVar(var);
142 |           }
143 |         }
144 |         break;
145 |     }
146 | 
147 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
148 |       traverseNode(c, n, scope);
149 |     }
150 |   }

359 |     // expects.
360 |     Pattern argPattern = Pattern.compile("(--[a-zA-Z_]+)=(.*)");

370 |         if (quotesMatcher.matches()) {
371 |           processedArgs.add(quotesMatcher.group(1));
372 |         } else {
373 |           processedArgs.add(value);
374 |         }

377 |       }

381 |     try {

387 |     }

===== M0567 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 90-387
Documentation:

Method code:
90 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 | 
93 |     if (type == Token.BLOCK) {
94 |       tryFoldBlock(t, n, parent);
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
99 |     if (left == null) {
100 |       return;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     if (type == Token.TYPEOF && NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left)) {
104 |       String newValue = null;
105 | 
106 |       switch (left.getType()) {
107 |         case Token.STRING:
108 |           newValue = "string";
109 |           break;
110 |         case Token.NUMBER:
111 |           newValue = "number";
112 |           break;
113 |         case Token.TRUE:
114 |         case Token.FALSE:
115 |           newValue = "boolean";
116 |           break;
117 |         case Token.NULL:
118 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
119 |         case Token.ARRAYLIT:
120 |           newValue = "object";
121 |           break;
122 |         case Token.NAME:
123 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the
124 |           // keyword undefined to something other than the value undefined.
125 |           if ("undefined".equals(left.getString())) {
126 |             newValue = "undefined";
127 |           }
128 |           break;
129 |       }
130 | 
131 |       if (newValue != null) {
132 |         parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newString(newValue));
133 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
134 |       }
135 | 
136 |       return;
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     if (type == Token.NOT ||
140 |         type == Token.NEG ||
141 |         type == Token.BITNOT) {
142 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
143 | 
144 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
145 |           // If the value of the NOT isn't used, then just throw
146 |           // away the operator
147 |           parent.replaceChild(n, n.removeFirstChild());
148 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
149 |           return;
150 |         }
151 | 
152 |         // Try to mimize NOT nodes such as !(x==y) into x!=y.
153 |         if (type == Token.NOT && tryMinimizeNot(t, n, parent)) {
154 |           return;
155 |         }
156 | 
157 |         if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left)) {
158 |           return;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         switch (type) {
162 |           case Token.NOT:
163 |             int result = NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(left) ? Token.FALSE :
164 |                          Token.TRUE;
165 |             parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(result));
166 |             t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
167 |             break;
168 | 
169 |           case Token.NEG:
170 |             try {
171 |               if (left.getType() == Token.NAME) {
172 |                 if (left.getString().equals("Infinity")) {
173 |                   // "-Infinity" is valid and a literal, don't modify it.
174 |                   return;
175 |                 } else if (left.getString().equals("NaN")) {
176 |                   // "-NaN" is "NaN".
177 |                   n.removeChild(left);
178 |                   parent.replaceChild(n, left);
179 |                   t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
180 |                   return;
181 |                 }
182 |               }
183 | 
184 |               double negNum = -left.getDouble();
185 |               parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(negNum));
186 |               t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
187 |             } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
188 |               // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
189 |               // user because they can't be doing anything good
190 |               error(t, NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
191 |             }
192 |             break;
193 | 
194 |           case Token.BITNOT:
195 |             try {
196 |               double val = left.getDouble();
197 |               if (val >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && val <= Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
198 |                 int intVal = (int) val;
199 |                 if (intVal == val) {
200 |                   parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(~intVal));
201 |                   t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
202 |                 } else {
203 |                   error(t, FRACTIONAL_BITWISE_OPERAND, left);
204 |                 }
205 |               } else {
206 |                 error(t, BITWISE_OPERAND_OUT_OF_RANGE, left);
207 |               }
208 |             } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
209 |               // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
210 |               // user because they can't be doing anything good
211 |               error(t, NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
212 |             }
213 |             break;
214 |         }
215 |         return;
216 |     } else if (type == Token.NEW) {
217 |       if (Token.NAME == left.getType()) {
218 |         String className = left.getString();
219 |         if ("RegExp".equals(className)) {
220 |           tryFoldRegularExpressionConstructor(t, n, parent);
221 |         } else if (left.getNext() == null) {
222 |           if ("Array".equals(className)) {
223 |             tryFoldLiteralConstructor(
224 |                 t, n, parent, className, Token.ARRAYLIT);
225 |           } else if ("Object".equals(className)) {
226 |             tryFoldLiteralConstructor(
227 |                 t, n, parent, className, Token.OBJECTLIT);
228 |           }
229 |         }
230 |       }
231 |     }
232 | 
233 |     if (type == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
234 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, left, n);
235 |       return;
236 |     }
237 | 
238 |     if (type == Token.RETURN) {
239 |       tryReduceReturn(t, n);
240 |       return;
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     Node right = left.getNext();
244 |     if (right == null) {
245 |       return;
246 |     }
247 | 
248 |     // TODO(johnlenz) Use type information if available to fold
249 |     // instanceof.
250 |     if (type == Token.INSTANCEOF
251 |         && NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left)
252 |         && !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(right)) {
253 |       if (NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(left)) {
254 |         // Non-object types are never instances.
255 |         parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(Token.FALSE));
256 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
257 |         return;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       if (right.getType() == Token.NAME
261 |           && "Object".equals(right.getString())) {
262 |         parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(Token.TRUE));
263 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
264 |         return;
265 |       }
266 |     }
267 | 
268 |     if (type == Token.IF || type == Token.HOOK) {
269 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, n.getFirstChild(), n);
270 |       boolean changes = tryFoldHookIf(t, n, parent);
271 | 
272 |       // bad cascades can occur if we run the second round
273 |       // of IF optimizations immediately
274 |       if (type == Token.IF && !changes) {
275 |         tryMinimizeIf(t, n, parent);
276 |       }
277 |       return;
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     if (type == Token.DO) {
281 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), n);
282 |       tryFoldDo(t, n, parent);
283 |       return;
284 |     }
285 | 
286 |     if (type == Token.WHILE) {
287 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), n);
288 |       tryFoldWhile(t, n, parent);
289 |       return;
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     if (type == Token.FOR) {
293 |       Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n);
294 |       if (condition != null) {
295 |         tryMinimizeCondition(t, condition, n);
296 |         // The root condition node might have changed, get it again.
297 |         condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n);
298 |         this.tryFoldForCondition(condition, n);
299 |       }
300 | 
301 |       tryFoldFor(t, n, parent);
302 |       return;
303 |     }
304 | 
305 |     if (type == Token.AND ||
306 |         type == Token.OR) {
307 |       tryFoldAndOr(t, n, left, right, parent);
308 |       return;
309 |     }
310 | 
311 |     if (type == Token.BITOR ||
312 |         type == Token.BITAND) {
313 |       tryFoldBitAndOr(t, n, left, right, parent);
314 |       return;
315 |     }
316 | 
317 |     if (type == Token.LSH ||
318 |         type == Token.RSH ||
319 |         type == Token.URSH) {
320 |       tryFoldShift(t, n, left, right, parent);
321 |       return;
322 |     }
323 | 
324 |     if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
325 |       tryFoldGetProp(t, n, left, right, parent);
326 |       return;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (type == Token.CALL) {
330 |       tryFoldStringJoin(t, n, left, right, parent);
331 |       tryFoldStringIndexOf(t, n, left, right, parent);
332 |       return;
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     if (type == Token.COMMA) {
336 |       tryFoldComma(t, n, left, right, parent);
337 |       return;
338 |     }
339 | 
340 |     if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
341 |       tryFoldAssign(t, n, left, right);
342 |     }
343 | 
344 |     if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left) ||
345 |         !NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right)) {
346 | 
347 |       if (type == Token.ADD)
348 |         tryFoldLeftChildAdd(t, n, left, right, parent);
349 | 
350 |       if (type == Token.LT ||
351 |           type == Token.GT) {
352 |         tryFoldComparison(t, n, left, right, parent);
353 |       }
354 | 
355 |       return; // The subsequent ops only work if the LHS & RHS are consts
356 |     }
357 | 
358 |     if (type == Token.ADD) {
359 |       tryFoldAdd(t, n, left, right, parent);
360 |       return;
361 |     }
362 |     if (type == Token.SUB ||
363 |         type == Token.MUL ||
364 |         type == Token.DIV) {
365 |       tryFoldArithmetic(t, n, left, right, parent);
366 |       return;
367 |     }
368 | 
369 |     if (type == Token.LT ||
370 |         type == Token.GT ||
371 |         type == Token.LE ||
372 |         type == Token.GE ||
373 |         type == Token.EQ ||
374 |         type == Token.NE ||
375 |         type == Token.SHEQ ||
376 |         type == Token.SHNE) {
377 |       tryFoldComparison(t, n, left, right, parent);
378 |       return;
379 |     }
380 | 
381 |     if (type == Token.GETELEM) {
382 |       tryFoldGetElem(t, n, left, right, parent);
383 |       return;
384 |     }
385 | 
386 |     // other types aren't handled
387 |   }

===== M0568 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseVariableDeclarations.ExploitAssigns.isCollapsibleValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseVariableDeclarations.java
Original range: 157-176
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether we know enough about the given value to be able
     * to collapse it into subsequent expressions.
     *
     * For example, we can collapse booleans and variable names:
     * <code>
     * x = 3; y = x; // y = x = 3;
     * a = true; b = true; // b = a = true;
     * <code>
     * But we won't try to collapse complex expressions.
     *
     * @param value The value node.
     * @param isLValue Whether it's on the left-hand side of an expr.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private boolean isCollapsibleValue(Node value, boolean isLValue) {
158 |       switch (value.getType()) {
159 |         case Token.GETPROP:
160 |           // Do not collapse GETPROPs on arbitrary objects, because
161 |           // they may be implemented  setter functions, and oftentimes
162 |           // setter functions fail on native objects. This is ok for "THIS"
163 |           // objects, because we assume that they are non-native.
164 |           return !isLValue || value.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS;
165 | 
166 |         case Token.NAME:
167 |         case Token.NUMBER:
168 |         case Token.TRUE:
169 |         case Token.FALSE:
170 |         case Token.NULL:
171 |         case Token.STRING:
172 |           return true;
173 |       }
174 | 
175 |       return false;
176 |     }

566 |   public Scope getScope() {
567 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
568 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
569 |       return scope;
570 |     }

Method code:
67 |   @Override
68 |   public void printSummary() {
69 |     if (summaryDetailLevel >= 3 ||
70 |         (summaryDetailLevel >= 1 && getErrorCount() + getWarningCount() > 0) ||
71 |         (summaryDetailLevel >= 2 && getTypedPercent() > 0.0)) {
72 |       if (getTypedPercent() > 0.0) {

75 |       } else {

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1395 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1396 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1397 |     if (typeExpr != null) {

1400 |         next();
1401 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1402 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1403 |           next();
1404 |         }
1405 |         skipEOLs();
1406 |         token = next();
1407 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);

738 |     public void removeProp(int propType)
739 |     {
740 |         PropListItem x = propListHead;

742 |             PropListItem prev = null;
743 |             while (x.type != propType) {

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 804-831
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
804 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
805 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
806 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
807 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
808 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
809 |           return;
810 |         }
811 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
812 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
813 | 
814 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
815 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
816 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
817 |           } else {
818 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
819 |           }
820 |         } else { // Has a catch.
821 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
822 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
823 |             return;
824 |           } else {
825 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
826 |           }
827 |         }
828 |         lastJump = handler;
829 |       }
830 |     }
831 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is

973 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {

===== M0625 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryMinimizeCondition(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1897-1960
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to minimize conditions expressions, as there are additional
   * assumptions that can be made when it is known that the final result
   * is a boolean.
   *
   * The following transformations are done recursively:
   *   !(x||y) --> !x&&!y
   *   !(x&&y) --> !x||!y
   *   !!x     --> x
   * Thus:
   *   !(x&&!y) --> !x||!!y --> !x||y
   */
Method code:
1897 |   void tryMinimizeCondition(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1898 | 
1899 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1900 |       case Token.NOT:
1901 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild();
1902 |         switch (first.getType()) {
1903 |           case Token.NOT: {
1904 |               Node newRoot = first.removeFirstChild();
1905 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
1906 |               n = newRoot; // continue from here.
1907 |               t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1908 | 
1909 |               // The child has moved up, to minimize it recurse.
1910 |               tryMinimizeCondition(t, n, parent);
1911 |               return;
1912 |             }
1913 |           case Token.AND:
1914 |           case Token.OR: {
1915 |               Node leftParent = first.getFirstChild();
1916 |               Node rightParent = first.getLastChild();
1917 |               if (leftParent.getType() != Token.NOT
1918 |                   || rightParent.getType() != Token.NOT) {
1919 |                 // No NOTs to elminate.
1920 |                 break;
1921 |               }
1922 |               Node left = leftParent.removeFirstChild();
1923 |               Node right = rightParent.removeFirstChild();
1924 | 
1925 |               int newOp = (first.getType() == Token.AND) ? Token.OR : Token.AND;
1926 |               Node newRoot = new Node(newOp, left, right);
1927 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
1928 |               n = newRoot; // continue from here.
1929 |               t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1930 | 
1931 |               // Unlike the NOT case above, we know that AND and OR are
1932 |               // valid root to check minimize so just break out and check
1933 |               // the children.
1934 |             }
1935 |             break;
1936 |         }
1937 |         break;
1938 | 
1939 |       case Token.OR:
1940 |       case Token.AND:
1941 |         // check the children.
1942 |         break;
1943 | 
1944 |       default:
1945 |         // if(true) --> if(1)
1946 |         if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(n)) {
1947 |           boolean result = NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(n);
1948 |           int equivalentResult = result ? 1 : 0;
1949 |           maybeReplaceChildWithNumber(t, n, parent, equivalentResult);
1950 |         }
1951 |         // We can't do anything else currently.
1952 |         return;
1953 |     }
1954 | 
1955 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; ) {
1956 |       Node next = c.getNext();  // c may be removed.
1957 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, c, n);
1958 |       c = next;
1959 |     }
1960 |   }

/**
     * shouldTraverse is call when descending into the Node tree, so it is used
     * here to build the context for label renames.
     *
     * {@inheritDoc}
     */
Method code:
117 |     public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node node,
118 |         Node parent) {
119 |       if (node.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
120 |         // Determine the new name for this label.
121 |         LabelNamespace current = namespaceStack.peek();
122 |         int currentDepth = current.renameMap.size() + 1;
123 |         String name = node.getFirstChild().getString();
124 |

128 |         current.renameMap.put(name, li);
129 |

131 |         if (names.size() < currentDepth) {
132 |           names.add(nameGenerator.generateNextName());
133 |         }
134 |

137 |       }

174 |   public List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> getDirectedGraphEdges(N n1, N n2) {
175 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = nodes.get(n1);
176 |     if (dNode1 == null) {

===== M0650 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 404-438
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
404 |     private void extractForInitializer(
405 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
406 | 
407 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
408 |         next = c.getNext();
409 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
410 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
411 |         switch (c.getType()) {
412 |           case Token.LABEL:
413 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
414 |             break;
415 |           case Token.FOR:
416 |             if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(c)
417 |                 && c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
418 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
419 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
420 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
421 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
422 | 
423 |               Node newStatement;
424 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
425 |               // but are handled differently.
426 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
427 |                 newStatement = init;
428 |               } else {
429 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
430 |               }
431 | 
432 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
433 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
434 |             }
435 |             break;
436 |         }
437 |       }
438 |     }

===== M0654 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 113-134
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   *
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 2 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. If A and B are in the same fixed point loop,
   * the loop will continue to run both A and B until both are finished
   * making changes.
   *
   * Other than that, the PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and
   * frequency of multi-run passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
113 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
114 |     Loop currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
115 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
116 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
117 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
118 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
119 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
120 | 
121 |           currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
122 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
123 |         }
124 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
125 |       } else {
126 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
127 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
128 |       }
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
132 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
133 |     }
134 |   }

Method code:
802 |     private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
803 |         int count = 0;
804 |         for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {

Method code:
125 |     @Override
126 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
127 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
128 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
129 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
130 |           return;
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
134 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
135 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
136 |         if (var != null) {
137 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         if ((info != null && info.isConstant()) &&
141 |             !n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
142 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
143 |           if (assertOnChange) {
144 |             String name = n.getString();
145 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
146 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
147 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
148 |                 "  gramps:" + n.getParent().getParent().toStringTree());
149 |           }
150 |           // Even though the AST has changed (an annotation was added),
151 |           // the annotations are not compared so don't report the change.
152 |           // reportCodeChange("constant annotation");
153 |         }
154 |       }
155 |     }

===== M0663 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 478-520
Documentation:

Method code:
478 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
479 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
480 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
481 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
482 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
483 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
484 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
485 |       return;
486 |     }
487 | 
488 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
489 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
490 | 
491 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
492 |     // function (unless it is called)
493 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
494 |       child = child.getNext();
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     if (child != null) {
498 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
499 |     } else {
500 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node));
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     // Synthetic blocks
504 |     if (parent != null) {
505 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
506 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
507 |         case Token.CASE:
508 |         case Token.TRY:
509 |           break;
510 |         default:
511 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
512 |             Node next = node.getLastChild();
513 |             if (next != null) {
514 |               createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFallThrough(next));
515 |             }
516 |           }
517 |           break;
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 888-1001
Documentation:

Method code:
888 |     private void toString(
889 |         StringBuilder sb,
890 |         boolean printSource,
891 |         boolean printAnnotations,
892 |         boolean printType)
893 |     {
894 |         if (Token.printTrees) {
895 |             sb.append(Token.name(type));
896 |             if (this instanceof StringNode) {
897 |                 sb.append(' ');
898 |                 sb.append(getString());
899 |             } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
900 |                 sb.append(' ');
901 |                 sb.append(first.getString());
902 |             } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
903 |                 ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode)this;
904 |                 if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
905 |                     FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode)this;
906 |                     sb.append(' ');
907 |                     sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
908 |                 }
909 |                 if (printSource) {
910 |                   sb.append(" [source name: ");
911 |                   sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
912 |                   sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
913 |                   sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd()
914 |                             - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
915 |                   sb.append("] [base line: ");
916 |                   sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
917 |                   sb.append("] [end line: ");
918 |                   sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
919 |                   sb.append(']');
920 |                 }
921 |             } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
922 |                 sb.append(' ');
923 |                 sb.append(getDouble());
924 |             }
925 |             if (printSource) {
926 |               int lineno = getLineno();
927 |               if (lineno != -1) {
928 |                   sb.append(' ');
929 |                   sb.append(lineno);
930 |               }
931 |             }
932 | 
933 |             if (printAnnotations) {
934 |               int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
935 |               for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
936 |                   int type = keys[i];
937 |                   PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
938 |                   sb.append(" [");
939 |                   sb.append(propToString(type));
940 |                   sb.append(": ");
941 |                   String value;
942 |                   switch (type) {
943 |                     case TARGETBLOCK_PROP : // can't add this as it recurses
944 |                       value = "target block property";
945 |                       break;
946 |                     case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP :     // can't add this as it is dull
947 |                       value = "last local block";
948 |                       break;
949 |                     case ISNUMBER_PROP:
950 |                       switch (x.intValue) {
951 |                         case BOTH:
952 |                           value = "both";
953 |                           break;
954 |                         case RIGHT:
955 |                           value = "right";
956 |                           break;
957 |                         case LEFT:
958 |                           value = "left";
959 |                           break;
960 |                         default:
961 |                           throw Kit.codeBug();
962 |                       }
963 |                       break;
964 |                     case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
965 |                       switch (x.intValue) {
966 |                         case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
967 |                           value = "eval";
968 |                           break;
969 |                         case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
970 |                           value = "with";
971 |                           break;
972 |                         default:
973 |                           // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
974 |                           throw Kit.codeBug();
975 |                       }
976 |                       break;
977 |                     default :
978 |                       Object obj = x.objectValue;
979 |                       if (obj != null) {
980 |                           value = obj.toString();
981 |                       } else {
982 |                           value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
983 |                       }
984 |                       break;
985 |                   }
986 |                   sb.append(value);
987 |                   sb.append(']');
988 |               }
989 |             }
990 | 
991 |             if (printType) {
992 |               if (jsType != null) {
993 |                   String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
994 |                   if (jsTypeString != null) {
995 |                       sb.append(" : ");
996 |                       sb.append(jsTypeString);
997 |                   }
998 |               }
999 |             }
1000 |         }
1001 |     }

119 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

56 |   CombinedCompilerPass(

151 |   public boolean equals(Object that) {
152 |     if (this == that) {
153 |       return true;
154 |     } else if (that instanceof JSType && this.isNominalType()) {
155 |       ObjectType thatObj = ObjectType.cast((JSType) that);

===== M0697 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 900-911
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
900 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
901 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
902 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
903 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
904 | 
905 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
906 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
907 | 
908 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
909 |         true);
910 |     return typeNode;
911 |   }

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 114-142
Documentation:

Method code:
114 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
115 |     boolean changed = false;
116 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
117 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
118 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
119 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
120 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
121 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
122 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
123 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
124 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
125 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
126 |         changed = changed ||
127 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
128 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
129 |       }
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     if (changed) {
133 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
134 |     }
135 | 
136 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
137 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
138 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
139 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
140 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
141 |     }
142 |   }
