===== M0713 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 247-331
Documentation:

Method code:
247 |     public  void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
248 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
249 |       if (refInfo != null) {
250 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
251 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
252 |         String fullName = name.fullName();
253 |         switch (ref.type) {
254 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
255 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
256 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
257 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
258 |             if (valParent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN && name.isSimpleName() &&
259 |                 name.declaration == ref) {
260 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
261 |               // before it's declared
262 |               compiler.report(
263 |                   JSError.make(t, val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
264 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
265 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
266 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
267 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
268 | 
269 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
270 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
271 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
272 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
273 |             }
274 |             break;
275 |           default:
276 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
277 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
278 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
279 |               // or it's an error.
280 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
281 |               if (info != null) {
282 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
283 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
284 |               }
285 |             }
286 |             break;
287 |         }
288 |       }
289 | 
290 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
291 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
292 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
293 |         compiler.report(
294 |             JSError.make(t, n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
298 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
299 |         if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
300 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
301 |           n.removeChild(last);
302 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
303 |         } else {
304 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.NAME);
305 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
306 |         }
307 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
308 |       }
309 | 
310 |       if (n.getType() == Token.CALL) {
311 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
312 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
313 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
314 |           //
315 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
316 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
317 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
318 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
319 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, expecially because
320 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
321 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
322 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
323 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
324 |           }
325 | 
326 |           assignableDefines.clear();
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
331 |     }

===== M0722 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 829-890
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
829 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
830 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
831 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
832 |                           String backslashEscape,
833 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
834 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
835 |     sb.append(quote);
836 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
837 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
838 |       switch (c) {
839 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
840 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
841 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
842 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
843 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
844 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
845 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
846 |           if (i >= 2 &&
847 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
848 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
849 |             sb.append("\\>");
850 |           } else {
851 |             sb.append(c);
852 |           }
853 |           break;
854 |         case '<':                       // Break </script into <\/script
855 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
856 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
857 |               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
858 |             sb.append("<\\");
859 |           } else {
860 |             sb.append(c);
861 |           }
862 |           break;
863 |         default:
864 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
865 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
866 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
867 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
868 |               sb.append(c);
869 |             } else {
870 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
871 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
872 |             }
873 |           } else {
874 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
875 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
876 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
877 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
878 |               sb.append(c);
879 |             } else {
880 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
881 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
882 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
883 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
884 |             }
885 |           }
886 |       }
887 |     }
888 |     sb.append(quote);
889 |     return sb.toString();
890 |   }

===== M0727 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameGenerator.generateNextName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameGenerator.java
Original range: 120-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Generates the next short name.
   */
Method code:
120 |   String generateNextName() {
121 |     while (true) {
122 |       String name = prefix;
123 |       
124 |       int i = nameCount;
125 |       
126 |       if (name.isEmpty()) {
127 |         int pos = i % firstChars.length;
128 |         name += firstChars[pos];
129 |         i /= firstChars.length;
130 |       }
131 | 
132 |       while (i > 0) {
133 |         i--;
134 |         int pos = i % nonFirstChars.length;
135 |         name += nonFirstChars[pos];
136 |         i /= nonFirstChars.length;
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       nameCount++;
140 |       
141 |       // Make sure it's not a JS keyword or reserved name.
142 |       if (TokenStream.isKeyword(name) || reservedNames.contains(name)) {
143 |         continue;
144 |       }
145 |       
146 |       return name;
147 |     }
148 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatement(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1019-1034
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
1019 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1020 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1021 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1022 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node, for instance,
1023 |     // is either part of an expression (as a anonymous function) or as
1024 |     // a statement.
1025 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1026 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1027 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1028 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1029 |       case Token.LABEL:
1030 |         return true;
1031 |       default:
1032 |         return false;
1033 |     }
1034 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 448-466
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
448 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
449 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
450 |         next = c.getNext();
451 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
452 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
453 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
454 |           }
455 | 
456 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
457 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
458 |             c.removeChild(name);
459 |             Node newVar = new Node(
460 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
461 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
462 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
463 |           }
464 |         }
465 |       }
466 |     }

===== M0761 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.LoopInternal.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 312-347
Documentation:

Method code:
312 |     public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
313 |       Preconditions.checkState(!loopMutex, "Nested loops are forbidden");
314 |       loopMutex = true;
315 |       if (randomizeLoops) {
316 |         randomizePasses();
317 |       } else {
318 |         optimizePasses();
319 |       }
320 | 
321 |       try {
322 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Use a smarter algorithm that dynamically adjusts
323 |         // the order that passes are run in.
324 |         int count = 0;
325 |         out: do {
326 |           if (count++ > MAX_LOOPS) {
327 |             compiler.throwInternalError(OPTIMIZE_LOOP_ERROR, null);
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           recentChange.reset();  // reset before this round of optimizations
331 | 
332 |           for (CompilerPass pass : myPasses) {
333 |             pass.process(externs, root);
334 |             if (hasHaltingErrors()) {
335 |               break out;
336 |             }
337 |           }
338 | 
339 |         } while (recentChange.hasCodeChanged() && !hasHaltingErrors());
340 | 
341 |         if (randomizeLoops) {
342 |           loopsRun.add(getPassOrder());
343 |         }
344 |       } finally {
345 |         loopMutex = false;
346 |       }
347 |     }

Original range: 251-294

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
251 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
252 |     this.type = type;
253 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
254 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
255 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
256 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {

258 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
259 |       int i = 0;

262 |         i++;
263 |       }

268 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.

275 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
276 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
277 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
278 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
279 |       trace.truncateEvents();
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
283 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
284 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
285 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
286 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
287 |                     + "try/finally block. "
288 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
289 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
290 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     trace.startEvent(this);
294 |   }

===== M0786 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1468-1493
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1468 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1469 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1470 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1471 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1472 |       skipEOLs();
1473 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1474 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1475 |       skipEOLs();
1476 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1477 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1478 |       skipEOLs();
1479 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1480 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1481 |       String string = stream.getString();
1482 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1483 |         skipEOLs();
1484 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1485 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1486 |         return newStringNode(string);
1487 |       } else {
1488 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1489 |       }
1490 |     }
1491 | 
1492 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1493 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Verifies that a method call has exactly one argument, and that it is of the
   * desired type. Reports a compile error if it doesn't.
   *
   * @return Whether the argument checked out okay
   */
Method code:
610 |   private boolean verifyArgument(NodeTraversal t, Node methodName, Node arg,
611 |       int desiredType) {
612 |     DiagnosticType diagnostic = null;
613 |     if (arg == null) {
614 |       diagnostic = NULL_ARGUMENT_ERROR;
615 |     } else if (arg.getType() != desiredType) {
616 |       diagnostic = INVALID_ARGUMENT_ERROR;
617 |     } else if (arg.getNext() != null) {
618 |       diagnostic = TOO_MANY_ARGUMENTS_ERROR;
619 |     }
620 |     if (diagnostic != null) {
621 |       compiler.report(
622 |           JSError.make(t, methodName,
623 |                        diagnostic, methodName.getQualifiedName()));
624 |       return false;
625 |     }
626 |     return true;
627 |   }

===== M0795 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/TokenStream.java
Original range: 296-321
Documentation:

Method code:
296 |     public static boolean isJSIdentifier(String s) {
297 |         int length = s.length();
298 | 
299 |         if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
300 |             return false;
301 | 
302 |         for (int i=1; i<length; i++) {
303 |             char c = s.charAt(i);
304 |             if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(c)) {
305 |                 if (c == '\\') {
306 |                     if (! ((i + 5) < length)
307 |                         && (s.charAt(i + 1) == 'u')
308 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 2), 0)
309 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 3), 0)
310 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 4), 0)
311 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 5), 0)) {
312 |                         return true;
313 |                      }
314 |                 }
315 | 
316 |                 return false;
317 |             }
318 |         }
319 | 
320 |         return true;
321 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if the current node's type implies side effects.
   *
   * This is a non-recursive version of the may have side effects
   * check; used to check wherever the current node's type is one of
   * the reason's why a subtree has side effects.
   */
Method code:
548 |   static boolean nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node n) {
549 |     if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n)) {
550 |       return true;
551 |     }
552 | 
553 |     switch(n.getType()) {
554 |       case Token.CALL:
555 |       case Token.DELPROP:
556 |       case Token.NEW:
557 |       case Token.DEC:
558 |       case Token.INC:
559 |       case Token.THROW:
560 |         return true;
561 |       case Token.NAME:
562 |         // A variable definition.
563 |         return n.hasChildren();
564 |       default:
565 |         return false;
566 |     }
567 |   }

===== M0814 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReplaceCssNames.Traversal.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReplaceCssNames.java
Original range: 112-162
Documentation:

Method code:
112 |     @Override
113 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
114 |       if (n.getType() == Token.CALL &&
115 |           GET_CSS_NAME_FUNCTION.equals(n.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName())) {
116 |         int count = n.getChildCount();
117 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
118 |         switch (count) {
119 |           case 2:
120 |             // Replace the function call with the processed argument.
121 |             if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
122 |               processStringNode(t, first);
123 |               n.removeChild(first);
124 |               parent.replaceChild(n, first);
125 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
126 |             } else {
127 |               compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, STRING_LITERAL_EXPECTED_ERROR,
128 |                   Token.name(first.getType())));
129 |             }
130 |             break;
131 | 
132 |           case 3:
133 |             // Replace function call with concatenation of two args.  It's
134 |             // assumed the first arg has already been processed.
135 | 
136 |             Node second = first.getNext();
137 | 
138 |             if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
139 |               compiler.report(JSError.make(
140 |                   t, n, UNEXPECTED_STRING_LITERAL_ERROR,
141 |                   first.getString(), second.getString()));
142 | 
143 |             } else if (second.getType() == Token.STRING) {
144 |               processStringNode(t, second);
145 |               n.removeChild(first);
146 |               Node replacement = new Node(Token.ADD, first,
147 |                   Node.newString("-" + second.getString()));
148 |               parent.replaceChild(n, replacement);
149 |               compiler.reportCodeChange();
150 | 
151 |             } else {
152 |               compiler.report(JSError.make(t, n, STRING_LITERAL_EXPECTED_ERROR,
153 |                   Token.name(second.getType())));
154 |             }
155 |             break;
156 | 
157 |           default:
158 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(
159 |                 t, n, INVALID_NUM_ARGUMENTS_ERROR, String.valueOf(count)));
160 |         }
161 |       }
162 |     }

===== M0820 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 806-833
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
806 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
807 |     // unknown
808 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
809 |       return true;
810 |     }
811 |     // equality
812 |     if (thisType.equals(thatType)) {
813 |       return true;
814 |     }
815 |     // all type
816 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
817 |       return true;
818 |     }
819 |     // unions
820 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
821 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
822 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
823 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
824 |           return true;
825 |         }
826 |       }
827 |     }
828 |     // named types
829 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
830 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
831 |     }
832 |     return false;
833 |   }

141 |   @Override
142 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     for (CallbackWrapper callback : callbacks) {
144 |       callback.shouldTraverseIfActive(t, n, parent);
145 |     }
146 |     // Note that this method could return false if all callbacks are inactive.

148 |     // in the typical case where not all nodes are inactive. It is

150 |     // time (indeed, there are several checking passes that never return false).

114 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
115 |     switch (n.getType()) {
116 |       case Token.VAR:
117 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
118 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
119 |              child != null;) {
120 |           Node next = child.getNext();
121 |           Preconditions.checkState(child.getType() == Token.NAME);
122 | 
123 |           String name = child.getString();
124 |           declareVar(name, child, n, parent, null, n);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionAnonymous(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(fnName, n.getFirstChild(), n, parent, null, n);
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext().getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var.getString(), var, n, parent, null, n);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal,Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 196-224
Documentation:
/**
     * For all variables in this scope, see if they are only used once.
     * If it looks safe to do so, inline them.
     */
Method code:
196 |     private void doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal t,
197 |         Map<Var, ReferenceCollection> referenceMap) {
198 | 
199 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
200 |         Var v = it.next();
201 | 
202 |         ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.get(v);
203 | 
204 |         // referenceInfo will be null if we're in constants-only mode
205 |         // and the variable is not a constant.
206 |         if (referenceInfo == null || isVarInlineForbidden(v) ||
207 |             staleVars.contains(v)) {
208 |           // Never try to inline exported variables or variables that
209 |           // were not collected or variables that have already been inlined.
210 |           continue;
211 |         } else if (isInlineableDeclaredConstant(v, referenceInfo)) {
212 |           Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReferenceForConstants();
213 |           Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
214 |           inlineDeclaredConstant(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
215 |           staleVars.add(v);
216 |         } else if (mode == Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
217 |           // If we're in constants-only mode, don't run more aggressive
218 |           // inlining heuristics. See InlineConstantsTest.
219 |           continue;
220 |         } else {
221 |           inlineNonConstants(v, referenceInfo);
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken,WhitespaceOption)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1112-1201
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1112 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1113 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1114 | 
1115 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1116 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1117 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1118 |     }
1119 | 
1120 |     stream.update();
1121 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1122 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1123 | 
1124 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1125 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1126 | 
1127 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1128 |       line = line.trim();
1129 |     }
1130 | 
1131 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1132 |     builder.append(line);
1133 | 
1134 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1135 |     token = next();
1136 | 
1137 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1138 | 
1139 |     do {
1140 |       switch (token) {
1141 |         case ANNOTATION:
1142 |         case EOC:
1143 |         case EOF:
1144 |           String multilineText = builder.toString();
1145 | 
1146 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1147 |             multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1148 |           }
1149 | 
1150 |           int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1151 |           int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1152 | 
1153 |           if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1154 |             jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1155 |                                   endLineno, endCharno);
1156 |           }
1157 | 
1158 |           return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1159 | 
1160 |         case STAR:
1161 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1162 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1163 |               builder.append(' ');
1164 |             }
1165 | 
1166 |             builder.append('*');
1167 |           }
1168 | 
1169 |           token = next();
1170 |           continue;
1171 | 
1172 |         case EOL:
1173 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1174 |             builder.append("\n");
1175 |           }
1176 | 
1177 |           ignoreStar = true;
1178 |           token = next();
1179 |           continue;
1180 | 
1181 |         default:
1182 |           ignoreStar = false;
1183 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1184 | 
1185 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1186 |             builder.append(' ');
1187 |           }
1188 | 
1189 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1190 | 
1191 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1192 | 
1193 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1194 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1195 |           }
1196 | 
1197 |           builder.append(line);
1198 |           token = next();
1199 |       }
1200 |     } while (true);
1201 |   }

===== M0875 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CoalesceVariableNames.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CoalesceVariableNames.java
Original range: 126-189
Documentation:

Method code:
126 |   @Override
127 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
128 |     if (colorings.isEmpty() || !NodeUtil.isName(n) ||
129 |         NodeUtil.isFunction(parent)) {
130 |       // Don't rename named functions.
131 |       return;
132 |     }
133 |     Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
134 |     GraphNode<Var, ?> vNode = colorings.peek().getGraph().getNode(var);
135 |     if (vNode == null) {
136 |       // This is not a local.
137 |       return;
138 |     }
139 |     Var coalescedVar = colorings.peek().getPartitionSuperNode(var);
140 |     
141 |     if (!usePseudoNames) {
142 |       if (vNode.getValue().equals(coalescedVar)) {
143 |         // The coalesced name is itself, nothing to do.
144 |         return;
145 |       }
146 | 
147 |       // Rename.
148 |       n.setString(coalescedVar.name);
149 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
150 | 
151 |       if (NodeUtil.isVar(parent)) {
152 |         removeVarDeclaration(n);
153 |       }
154 |     } else {
155 |       // This code block is slow but since usePseudoName is for debugging,
156 |       // we should not sacrifice performance for non-debugging compilation to
157 |       // make this fast.
158 |       String pseudoName = null;
159 |       Set<String> allMergedNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
160 |       for (Iterator<Var> i = t.getScope().getVars(); i.hasNext();) {
161 |         Var iVar = i.next();
162 |         
163 |         // Look for all the variables that can be merged (in the graph by now)
164 |         // and it is merged with the current coalscedVar.
165 |         if (colorings.peek().getGraph().getNode(iVar) != null &&
166 |             coalescedVar.equals(colorings.peek().getPartitionSuperNode(iVar))) {
167 |           allMergedNames.add(iVar.name);
168 |         }
169 |       }
170 |       
171 |       // Keep its original name.
172 |       if (allMergedNames.size() == 1) {
173 |         return;
174 |       }
175 |       
176 |       pseudoName = Joiner.on("_").join(allMergedNames);
177 |       
178 |       while (t.getScope().isDeclared(pseudoName, true)) {
179 |         pseudoName += "$";
180 |       }
181 |       
182 |       n.setString(pseudoName);
183 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
184 | 
185 |       if (!vNode.getValue().equals(coalescedVar) && NodeUtil.isVar(parent)) {
186 |         removeVarDeclaration(n);
187 |       }
188 |     }
189 |   }

===== M0883 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 213-288
Documentation:

Method code:
213 |   @Override
214 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
215 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
216 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
217 | 
218 |     switch (n.getType()) {
219 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
220 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
221 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
222 |           return true;
223 |         }
224 |         return false;
225 |       case Token.TRY:
226 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
227 |         return true;
228 |     }
229 | 
230 |     /*
231 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
232 |      * is.
233 |      *
234 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
235 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
236 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
237 |      *
238 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
239 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
240 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
241 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
242 |      *
243 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
244 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
245 |      */
246 |     if (parent != null) {
247 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
248 |         case Token.FOR:
249 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
250 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
251 | 
252 |         // Skip the conditions.
253 |         case Token.IF:
254 |         case Token.WHILE:
255 |         case Token.WITH:
256 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
257 |         case Token.DO:
258 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
259 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
260 |         case Token.SWITCH:
261 |         case Token.CASE:
262 |         case Token.CATCH:
263 |         case Token.LABEL:
264 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
265 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
266 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
267 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
268 |         case Token.BREAK:
269 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
270 |         case Token.VAR:
271 |         case Token.RETURN:
272 |         case Token.THROW:
273 |           return false;
274 |         case Token.TRY:
275 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
276 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
277 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
278 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
279 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
280 |            */
281 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
282 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
283 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
284 |           }
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 |     return true;
288 |   }

264 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
265 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
266 |       return;
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     try {
270 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
271 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
272 | 
273 |       sourceName = "";
274 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
275 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
276 |

279 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);

===== M0887 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RenameVars.assignNames(Set<Assignment>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RenameVars.java
Original range: 389-461
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines which new names to substitute for the original names.
   */
Method code:
389 |   private void assignNames(Set<Assignment> varsToRename) {
390 |     NameGenerator globalNameGenerator =
391 |         new NameGenerator(reservedNames, prefix, reservedCharacters);
392 | 
393 |     // Local variables never need a prefix.
394 |     NameGenerator localNameGenerator = prefix.isEmpty() ?
395 |         globalNameGenerator : new NameGenerator(reservedNames, "",
396 |         reservedCharacters);
397 | 
398 |     // Generated names and the assignments for non-local vars.
399 |     List<Assignment> pendingAssignments = new ArrayList<Assignment>();
400 |     List<String> generatedNamesForAssignments = new ArrayList<String>();
401 | 
402 |     for (Assignment a : varsToRename) {
403 |       if (a.newName != null) {
404 |         continue;
405 |       }
406 | 
407 |       if (externNames.contains(a.oldName)) {
408 |         continue;
409 |       }
410 | 
411 |       String newName;
412 |       if (a.oldName.startsWith(LOCAL_VAR_PREFIX)) {
413 |         // For local variable, we make the assignment right away.
414 |         newName = localNameGenerator.generateNextName();
415 |         finalizeNameAssignment(a, newName);
416 |       } else {
417 |         // For non-local variable, delay finalizing the name assignment
418 |         // until we know how many new names we'll have of length 2, 3, etc.
419 |         newName = globalNameGenerator.generateNextName();
420 |         pendingAssignments.add(a);
421 |         generatedNamesForAssignments.add(newName);
422 |       }
423 |       reservedNames.add(newName);
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Now that we have a list of generated names, and a list of variable
427 |     // Assignment objects, we assign the generated names to the vars as
428 |     // follows:
429 |     // 1) The most frequent vars get the shorter names.
430 |     // 2) If N number of vars are going to be assigned names of the same
431 |     //    length, we assign the N names based on the order at which the vars
432 |     //    first appear in the source. This makes the output somewhat less
433 |     //    random, because symbols declared close together are assigned names
434 |     //    that are quite similar. With this heuristic, the output is more
435 |     //    compressible.
436 |     //    For instance, the output may look like:
437 |     //    var da = "..", ea = "..";
438 |     //    function fa() { .. } function ga() { .. }
439 | 
440 |     int numPendingAssignments = generatedNamesForAssignments.size();
441 |     for (int i = 0; i < numPendingAssignments;) {
442 |       SortedSet<Assignment> varsByOrderOfOccurrence =
443 |           new TreeSet<Assignment>(ORDER_OF_OCCURRENCE_COMPARATOR);
444 | 
445 |       // Add k number of Assignment to the set, where k is the number of
446 |       // generated names of the same length.
447 |       int len = generatedNamesForAssignments.get(i).length();
448 |       for (int j = i;
449 |            j < numPendingAssignments &&
450 |                generatedNamesForAssignments.get(j).length() == len;
451 |            j++) {
452 |         varsByOrderOfOccurrence.add(pendingAssignments.get(j));
453 |       }
454 | 
455 |       // Now, make the assignments
456 |       for (Assignment a : varsByOrderOfOccurrence) {
457 |         finalizeNameAssignment(a, generatedNamesForAssignments.get(i));
458 |         ++i;
459 |       }
460 |     }
461 |   }

115 |   public void exitScope(NodeTraversal traversal) {
116 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(traversal);
117 | 
118 |     // This is the case when we are exiting the global scope where we had never
119 |     // collected argument access list. Since we do not perform this optimization
120 |     // for the global scope, we will skip this exit point.
121 |     if (currentArgumentsAccess == null) {
122 |       return;
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     // Attempt to replace the argument access and if the AST has been change,
126 |     // report back to the compiler.
127 |     if (tryReplaceArguments(traversal.getScope())) {
128 |       traversal.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     // After the attempt to replace the arguments. The currentArgumentsAccess
132 |     // is stale and as we exit the Scope, no longer holds all the access to the
133 |     // current scope anymore. We'll pop the access list from the outer scope
134 |     // and set it as currentArgumentsAcess if the outer scope is not the global
135 |     // scope.
136 |     if (!argumentsAccessStack.isEmpty()) {
137 |       currentArgumentsAccess = argumentsAccessStack.pop();
138 |     } else {
139 |       currentArgumentsAccess = null;
140 |     }

   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
606 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
607 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
608 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
609 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
610 |                                  boolean validation) {
611 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
612 |     CodeConsumer cp =
613 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
614 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
615 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap)
616 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap);
617 |     CodeGenerator cg =
618 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
619 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(cp, outputCharset)
620 |         : new CodeGenerator(cp, outputCharset, validation);
621 |     cg.add(root);
622 | 
623 |     String code = ((HasGetCode) cp).getCode();
624 | 
625 |     if (createSourceMap) {
626 |       cp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
627 |     }
628 | 
629 |     return code;
630 |   }

===== M0909 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 302-310
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
302 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
303 |       Node ret = transform(n);
304 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
305 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
306 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
307 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
308 |       }
309 |       return ret;
310 |     }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
66 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
67 |     return s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q");
68 |   }

===== M0916 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.collectDefines(Node,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 153-181
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
153 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
154 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
155 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
156 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
157 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
158 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
159 |         allDefines.add(name);
160 |       } else if (name.refs != null) {
161 |         for (Ref ref : name.refs) {
162 |           Node n = ref.node;
163 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
164 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
165 |           if (info == null &&
166 |               parent.getType() == Token.VAR && parent.hasOneChild()) {
167 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
168 |           }
169 | 
170 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
171 |             allDefines.add(name);
172 |             break;
173 |           }
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
179 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
180 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
181 |   }

   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and

   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.

115 |       return built;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 149-194
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
149 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
150 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
151 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
152 |       case Token.BLOCK:
153 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
154 |       case Token.TRY:
155 |       case Token.FINALLY:
156 |         return true;
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
159 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
160 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
161 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
162 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
163 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
164 |         // child.
165 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
166 |       case Token.WHILE:
167 |       case Token.DO:
168 |       case Token.IF:
169 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
170 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
171 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
172 |         
173 |       case Token.FOR:
174 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
175 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
176 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
177 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
178 |         // That way the following:
179 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
180 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
181 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
182 |           return n == parent.getLastChild(); 
183 |         } else {
184 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
185 |         }
186 |       case Token.SWITCH:
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.WITH:
190 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
191 |       default:
192 |         return false;
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0946 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

===== M0952 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-81
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     if (!Character.isUpperCase(name.charAt(0))) {
76 |       return false;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // hack way of checking that there aren't any lower-case letters
80 |     return name.toUpperCase().equals(name);
81 |   }

338 |   protected int getChar() {
339 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
340 |       cursor++;
341 |       --ungetCursor;
342 |       if (charno == -1) {
343 |         charno = getOffset();
344 |       }
345 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     for(;;) {
349 |       int c;
350 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
351 |         hitEOF = true;
352 |         if (charno == -1) {
353 |           charno = getOffset();
354 |         }
355 |         return EOF_CHAR;
356 |       }
357 |       cursor++;
358 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
359 | 
360 | 
361 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
362 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
363 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
364 |           continue;
365 |         }
366 |         lineEndChar = -1;
367 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
368 |         lineno++;
369 |       }
370 | 
371 |       if (c <= 127) {
372 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
373 |           lineEndChar = c;
374 |           c = '\n';
375 |         }
376 |       } else {
377 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
378 |           continue;
379 |         }
380 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
381 |           lineEndChar = c;
382 |           c = '\n';
383 |         }
384 |       }
385 | 
386 |       if (charno == -1) {
387 |         charno = getOffset();
388 |       }
389 | 
390 |       return c;
391 |     }
392 |   }

===== M0965 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 129-175
Documentation:

Method code:
129 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
130 |                                    String sourceString,
131 |                                    Config config,
132 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
133 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
134 |         config, errorReporter);
135 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
136 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
137 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
138 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
139 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
140 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
141 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
142 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
143 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
144 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
145 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
146 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
147 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
148 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
149 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
150 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
151 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
152 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
153 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
154 |           } else {
155 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
156 |             if (info != null) {
157 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
158 |             }
159 |           }
160 |         }
161 |       }
162 | 
163 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
164 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
165 |       // to that node.
166 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
167 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
168 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
169 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
170 |         }
171 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |     return irNode;
175 |   }

===== M0991 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 49-120
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
50 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
51 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
52 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
53 |     // so we'll allow it.
54 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EMPTY ||
55 |         n.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
56 |       return;
57 |     }
58 | 
59 |     if (parent == null)
60 |       return;
61 | 
62 |     int pt = parent.getType();
63 |     if (pt == Token.COMMA) {
64 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
65 |       if (gramps.getType() == Token.CALL &&
66 |           parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
67 |         // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
68 |         // call to an eval. See Ecma-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's ok for the first
69 |         // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
70 |         // an eval.
71 |         if (n == parent.getFirstChild() &&
72 |             parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
73 |             n.getNext().getType() == Token.NAME &&
74 |             "eval".equals(n.getNext().getString())) {
75 |           return;
76 |         }
77 |       }
78 | 
79 |       if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
80 |         for (Node an : parent.getAncestors()) {
81 |           int ancestorType = an.getType();
82 |           if (ancestorType == Token.COMMA)
83 |             continue;
84 |           if (ancestorType != Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
85 |               ancestorType != Token.BLOCK)
86 |             return;
87 |           else
88 |             break;
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     } else if (pt != Token.EXPR_RESULT && pt != Token.BLOCK) {
92 |       if (pt == Token.FOR && parent.getChildCount() == 4 &&
93 |           (n == parent.getFirstChild() ||
94 |            n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext())) {
95 |         // Fall through and look for warnings for the 1st and 3rd child
96 |         // of a for.
97 |       } else {
98 |         return;  // it might be ok to not have a side-effect
99 |       }
100 |     }
101 |     if (NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType()) ||
102 |         !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n)) {
103 |       if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
104 |         // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
105 |         // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
106 |         return;
107 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(n)) {
108 |         // we already reported the problem when we visited the child.
109 |         return;
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
113 |       if (n.getType() == Token.STRING) {
114 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
115 |       }
116 | 
117 |       t.getCompiler().report(
118 |           JSError.make(t, n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
119 |     }
120 |   }

===== M0995 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JSModuleGraph.JSModuleGraph(JSModule[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JSModuleGraph.java
Original range: 72-95
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a module graph from a list of modules in dependency order.
   */
Method code:
72 |   public JSModuleGraph(JSModule[] modulesInDepOrder) {
73 |     moduleDepths = new HashMap<JSModule, Integer>(modulesInDepOrder.length);
74 |     modulesByDepth = new ArrayList<List<JSModule>>();
75 | 
76 |     for (JSModule module : modulesInDepOrder) {
77 |       int depth = 0;
78 |       for (JSModule dep : module.getDependencies()) {
79 |         Integer depDepth = moduleDepths.get(dep);
80 |         if (depDepth == null) {
81 |           throw new ModuleDependenceException(String.format(
82 |               "Modules not in dependency order: %s preceded %s",
83 |               module.getName(), dep.getName()),
84 |               module, dep);
85 |         }
86 |         depth = Math.max(depth, depDepth + 1);
87 |       }
88 | 
89 |       moduleDepths.put(module, depth);
90 |       if (depth == modulesByDepth.size()) {
91 |         modulesByDepth.add(new ArrayList<JSModule>());
92 |       }
93 |       modulesByDepth.get(depth).add(module);
94 |     }
95 |   }

===== M1005 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 151-227
Documentation:
/**
     *
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
151 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
152 |       int nType = n.getType();
153 |       switch (nType) {
154 |         case Token.NAME:
155 |         case Token.STRING:
156 |           String nString = n.getString();
157 |           if (nType == Token.NAME &&
158 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL &&
159 |               "eval".equals(nString)) {
160 |             n.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
161 |           }
162 |           if (convention.isConstant(nString)) {
163 |             n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
164 |           }
165 |           break;
166 | 
167 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
168 |           JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
169 |           if (fnInfo == null) {
170 |             // Look for the info on other nodes.
171 |             if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
172 |               // on ASSIGNs
173 |               fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
174 |             } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
175 |               // on var NAME = function() { ... };
176 |               fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
177 |             }
178 |           }
179 | 
180 |           // Compute which function parameters are optional and
181 |           // which are var_args.
182 |           Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
183 |           for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
184 |                arg != null;
185 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
186 |             String argName = arg.getString();
187 |             JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
188 |                 null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
189 | 
190 |             if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
191 |                 typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
192 |               arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
193 |             }
194 |             if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
195 |                 typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
196 |               arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
197 |             }
198 |           }
199 |           break;
200 | 
201 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
202 |           if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
203 |             for (Node key = n.getFirstChild();
204 |                  key != null; key = key.getNext().getNext()) {
205 |               Node value = key.getNext();
206 |               if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
207 |                   key.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
208 |                 value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
209 |               }
210 |             }
211 |           }
212 |           break;
213 |       }
214 | 
215 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Determine if it is possible to simply use the javadoc
216 |       // everywhere rather than use IS_DISPATCHER.
217 |       /*
218 |        * Translate dispatcher info into the property expected node.
219 |        */
220 |       if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isJavaDispatch()) {
221 |         if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
222 |           Node fnNode = n.getLastChild();
223 |           Preconditions.checkState(fnNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
224 |           fnNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_DISPATCHER, true);
225 |         }
226 |       }
227 |     }
